董事长、党委书记 杲传勇

Board Chairman and Party Secretary Gao Chuanyong

# 董事长致辞

2014年，面对严峻复杂的经济形势和日趋激烈的竞争环境，齐商银行在各级党委政府及监管部门的正确领导和指导支持下，以锐意进取的精神和扎实稳健的作风，有效落实“稳增长、促转型、防风险、提效益”的经营原则，坚定发展信心，坚守市场定位，坚持转型发展，克服了经济形势变化及金融政策改革带来的困难和挑战，继续保持了稳中有升、稳中向好的发展态势，全行管理水平和经营业绩迈上了一个新层次。截止2014年末，全行资产总额达到707.3亿元，较年初增长15.8%；各项存款余额589.3亿元，较年初增加13.9%，淄博地区市场份额居第四位，存款总量及增量均居全省城商行第三位；各项贷款余额382.5亿元，较年初增加14.5%；实现利润总额10.3亿元，净利润7.6亿元；资产利润率达到1.16%，资本利润率达到14.17%；各项主要监管指标持续达标，实现了规模、质量与效益的协调发展。

一治理结构持续优化。2014年，本行董事会修订完善授权方案，监事会修订《工作实施细则》，重新梳理并汇编形成公司治理层面的制度库，为规范有效履职奠定制度基础；持续实施董事会月度重点工作部署制度，按月对经营层需重点关注及落实事项进行部署安排，监事会定期对经营层贯彻落实情况进行监督评价，重点工作均得到有效落实；资本管理不断加强，采取股票股利的形式完成了2013年度红利分配工作，并以3.3元的价格增发3亿股资本金，进一步壮大了资本实力，提高了资本充足水平；依法合规完成了第六届董监事会的换届工作，引进了具备专业水平的高层次独立董事和外部监事，公司治理的规范性和有效性进一步增强。

一转型创新持续深化。威海分行及7家支行、首家社区银行如期开业，全行营业网点达到99家，异地分行存款及利润贡献度达到30%。网点转型成功试点并稳步推进，17家试点网点营销模式不断优化，团队素质及服务水平明显提升。小微金融、零售金融、国际金融、同业金融等条线产品不断推陈出新，业务规模不断壮大，经营效益日益提升，全行个人消费贷款余额占比、高净值财富客户数量、中间业务收入占比都超额完成了计划目标。国内首家致力于提供小微企业、三农产业、青年和妇女创业等普惠金融服务的互联网投融资平台-“齐乐融融E平台”上线运行，为解决中小微企业“融资难、融资贵”问题开辟了新渠道。截至年末，全行小微企业贷款余额242.4亿元，较年初增加57.7亿元，小微企业贷款户数达6,577户，新增1,654户，较好地完成了“两个不低于”监管指标，获评“2014全国支持中小企业发展十佳商业银行”、“山东省银行业服务三农十佳金融产品奖”等称号。

一内控机制持续细化。聘请知名咨询机构实施了全面风险管理体系评估规划项目，风险管理的全面性和专业性进一步提高；组织开展了全行重要信息系统、同城灾备切换和流动性风险实战式应急演练，得到了银监会和各级监管部门的高度评价；深化会计结算辅导，加强条线案防及操作风险飞行检查，柜面操作风险防范能力持续增强；丰富舆情监测手段，及时化解诱发因素，有效防范了声誉风险；开展了市场风险、信息科技风险专项审计，保障了审计成果的有效应用，稽核内审“第三道防线”职能得到有效发挥。

一作风建设持续强化。深入扎实开展了党的群众路线教育实践活动，全行风清气正、干事创业的良好氛围得到巩固。积极履行社会责任，得到了各级政府的高度评价。“齐银学院”正式投入使用，成为全行干部员工职业养成、素质提升、成长成材的基地和“摇篮”。推出了“齐娃”吉祥物，对外宣传力度不断加大，文明规范服务水平持续提高，多家分支机构获评“2014年度中国银行业文明规范服务千佳示范单位”暨“中国银行业文明规范服务五星级营业网点”、全国及省级文明规范服务示范单位荣誉称号。“齐银文化”的影响力和凝聚力持续增强，荣获了“践行社会主义核心价值观企业文化模范单位”、“全市银行业金融机构年度考核先进单位”等称号，全行的品牌形象和社会美誉度进一步提升。

2015年，国际国内经济形势依然复杂多变。本行将全面贯彻落实党的十八大、十八届三中、四中全会和中央经济工作会议精神，坚持“稳中求进”的总基调和“稳增长、提质量、调结构、增效益、建机制、促转型”的总原则，突出重点、破解难点、抓出亮点，紧紧围绕“市民银行”和“中小企业主办行”的市场定位，切实增强对宏观经济政策的预见性和研判力，正确认识新常态，主动适应新常态，努力引领新常态，提升风险管理，深化转型发展，创新服务手段，增强核心竞争力，实现新常态下的科学稳健、可持续发展，为将本行打造成为一家特色显著、效益优良、省内领先的区域性现代化商业银行、实现新时代的“齐银梦”而不懈奋斗！

## President's Address

In 2014, in the face of severe and complex economic trend and increasingly competitive environment with each passing day, under the correct leadership of the party committees and governments at all levels and strongly support from all sectors of society, Qishang Bank earnestly adopted forging spirit and strong, steady and sound style, effectively implemented business principles of "steady growth, transfer promotion, risk prevention and benefit enhancement", insisted on development confidence, adhered to market location, persisted in transformation and development and overcome the difficulties and challenges brought by the change of economic situation and reform of financial policy, continually remained the steady, promoting and sound development trend. The management level and business performance of the bank stepped on a new level. By the end of 2014, the total asset of the bank reached RMB 70.73 billion, increasing by 15.8% when compared with that at the beginning of the year; the balance of various deposits was RMB 58.93 billion, increasing by 13.9% when compared with that at the beginning of the year; the market share of Zibo ranked the fourth place, the total deposit and increment ranked the third place among the commercial banks over the province; the balance of various loans was RMB 38.25 billion, increasing by 14.5% when compared with that at the beginning of the year; the total realized profit was RMB 1.03 billion and the net profit was RMB 760 million; the return on assets was up to 1.16% and the return on capital was up to 14.17%; various main supervision indicators were continually satisfied, thus, a harmonious development of scale, quality and benefits was realized.

— Governance structure was continually improved. In 2014, the Board of Directors of the bank revised and improved the authorization scheme and the Board of Supervisors revised Work Implementing Rules, refilled and compiled the system database of company governance level to establish the system foundation for specifying the effective performance of the duties and responsibilities; the deployment of monthly key work of the Board of Directors was continually implemented and the matters of the operating level that shall be given key focuses and implementation monthly was scheduled and settled; the Board of Supervisors performed regular supervision and assessment to the implementation of the operating level and key work has been effectively implemented; capital management was increasingly strengthened; 2013 dividend distribution was completed in the way of stock dividend and capital fund of RMB 300 million was increased and issued at a price of RMB 3.3 per share to further expand the capital strength and enhance the capital adequacy level; the replacement of the sixth Board of Directors and the sixth Board of Supervisors has been completed in accordance with laws and regulations; high-level independent directors and external supervisors with professional level were introduced in and the normalization and effectiveness of corporate governance were further strengthened.

—Transformation and innovation were continually deepened. Weihai Branch and 7 Sub-branches and the first community bank were opened as scheduled. As a result, there are 99 business sites of the bank and the deposit and profit contribution of branches of other place has reached 30%. The site transfer was successful and was steadily promoted. 17 pilot sites unceasingly improved the marketing mode and the group qualities and service level were obviously promoted. Line products such as micro-sized finance, retail finance, international finance and inter-bank finance were continually innovated and launched; the business scale was continually expanded and operating income was promoted with each passing day; the individual consumption loan balance ratio, quantity of high-net value clients and ratio of income from intermediary business overfilled the planned target. The first internet investment and financing platform devoted to provide finance service to micro-sized enterprise, enterprises for agriculture, farmer and rural area, youth and women pioneering at home—“Qilerongrong E Platform” was listed and run, which created new channel for resolving the problems of "difficulty financing" and "costly financing" of micro-sized and medium enterprises. By the end of the year, the loan balance of the micro-sized and medium enterprises of the bank was RMB 24.24 billion, increasing by RMB 5.77 billion when compared with that at the beginning of the year; the quantity of loan clients of micro-sized and medium enterprises reached 6,577, increasing by 1,654; the bank has well completed the "two not-less-than" supervision indicators and was honored with titles such as "2014 National Top 10 Commercial Bank Supporting the Development of Small and Medium Enterprises" and "Top 10 Financial Products for Agriculture, Farmer and Rural Area of Shandong Banking Service".

—Internal control mechanism was continually refined. Famous consulting organization was employed to implement overall risk management system assessment and planning project and the comprehensiveness and specialty of risk management was further enhanced; emergency drills in regards to the important information system, city-wide exchange of backup for disaster recovery and liquidity risk of the bank were organized and carried out, which received highly thinking of CRBC and supervising departments of all levels; accounting settlement tutorship was deepened, the flight check in regards to line case protection and operation risk was strengthened and the counter operation risk prevention capacity was continually enhanced; public opinion supervision method was enriched, inducing factor was timely eliminated and reputation risk was effectively prevented; special audit of market risk and information science and technology risk was developed, the effective application of the auditing result was guaranteed and the function of "the third protection line" in internal audit was effectively fulfilled.

—The work style construction was continually strengthened. The education and practice activity of the mass line of the party was deeply and steadily carried out and the good atmosphere of the bank was consolidated; the social responsibilities were actively performed, and highly thinking of the governments of all levels was received; "Qishang Bank College" was formally put into use, which became the base and "cradle" of the cadres and employees of the bank for occupation cultivation, quality promotion and growth. The bank launched "Qiwa" mascot, increased the publicity to the public, and continually enhanced the civilized and specified service level. Multiple branches and sub-branches were honored with "2014 Top1000 Civilized and Specified Service Demonstration Company of Chinese Banking Industry" and "5-Star Civilized and Specified Service Business Site of Chinese Banking Industry" and national and provincial civilized and specified service demonstration company. The influence and cohesion of "Qishang Bank Culture" became stronger continually and Qishang Bank was honored with titles such as "Example Company of Practicing Socialist Core Values Enterprise Culture" and "Advanced Financial Organization in Municipal Banking Cross-year Assessment". The brand image and social reputation of the bank were further promoted.

In 2015, the international and domestic economic situation is still complex and full of changes. The bank will comprehensively carry out and implement the spirit of the 18th National Congress of Communist Party, the Third and Fourth Plenary Sessions of the 18th National Congress of Communist Party and the Central Economic Work Conference, insist on the general keynote of "obtaining progress steadily" and general principles of "steady growth, quality promotion, structure adjustment, benefit enhancement, mechanism establishment and urging transfer", emphasize key points, break difficulties, search out highlights, closely adhere to the market position of "citizen bank" and "banks in charge of small and medium enterprises", practically strengthen the forecast and research and judgment capacity of macroeconomic policy. We will also correctly understand new status, actively adapt to new status, endeavor to leading new status, advance risk management, deepen transformation and development, innovate service methods, heighten core competence, realize scientific, steady, sound and sustainable development under new status and strive for building the bank into a regional modernization commercial bank with distinctive features, good benefits and leading superiority and realizing the dream of Qishang Bank of a new era!

1、资产规模继续保持较快增长。截至报告期末，本行集团口径资产总额707.34亿元，较年初增加96.66亿元，增幅15.83%；母公司法人口径资产总额698.73亿元，较年初增加91.41亿元，增幅15.05%；集团口径贷款总额382.53亿元，较年初增加48.29亿元，增幅14.45%；母公司法人口径贷款总额375.80亿元，较年初增加44.79亿元，增幅13.53%。

2、盈利水平增势趋缓。截至报告期末，集团口径实现税前利润10.31亿元，较同期减少0.01亿元，减幅0.13%；母公司法人口径实现税前利润10.14亿元，较同期减少0.14亿元，减幅1.33%；集团口径实现净利润7.63亿元，较同期减少0.30亿元，减幅3.84%；母公司法人口径实现净利润7.58亿元，较同期减少0.34亿元，减幅4.27%。集团口径资本利润率为14.17%，资产利润率为1.16%。

3、成本控制在合理水平。报告期内，集团口径成本收入比为36.70%，较同期下降0.29个百分点，其中，发生业务及管理费用9.09亿元，比上年同期增长13.79%；营业收入24.77亿元，比上年同期增长14.71%；母公司法人口径成本收入比为36.64%，较同期下降0.03个百分点。其中，发生业务及管理费用8.90亿元，比上年同期增长13.39%；营业收入24.30亿元，比上年同期增长13.48%。

4、经营风险控制有效。报告期内，面对国内外严峻的经济形势，本行积极采取措施，聘请了知名咨询机构实施全面风险管理体系评估规划项目，实行了条线嵌入式风险管理模式，风险管理全面性和专业性进一步提高；强化授信风险管控，加强产业政策和行业研究，改善信贷投向，重视发展中小企业贷款，授信集中度进一步下降，信贷结构持续优化；加大不良资产清收处置力度，积极提升信贷资产质量。截至报告期末，五级分类口径不良贷款率1.90%，较年初下降0.05个百分点；拨备覆盖率155.62%，较去年同期下降14.72个百分点，资本充足率11.96%，核心一级资本充足率11.23%，分别较去年同期增长1.1和1.4个百分点。截至报告期末，本行集团口径杠杆率为6.54%，一级资本净额为62.68亿元，调整后表内资产余额706.94亿元、调整后表外资产余额251.65亿元。

5、机构发展战略顺利实施。报告期内，威海分行、西安西大街支行、西安高新科技支行、滨州无棣支行、东营西城支行、东营垦利支行、潍坊高密支行、高青芦湖支行、滨州胜滨社区支行相继开业，省内机构建设步伐不断加快，省外机构辐射面积不断扩大。

6、改革创新成效显著。报告期内，制度建设持续加强，各项业务规范发展，产品创新深入推进；产品开发效率进一步提高，表外业务进一步拓展，新产品推广应用日益广泛；理财产品日趋丰富，满足了投资者多元化需求；加快小企业金融服务中心和社区支行建设，加大市场营销力度，差异化、特色化经营迈出新步伐。

## 二、主要业务运作情况

1、个人存款情况：截至报告期末，本行本外币个人存款余额191.61亿元，比年初增加28.49亿元，增幅17.47%，在各项存款中占比32.51%，比年初上升0.96个百分点。

2、个人贷款情况：截至报告期末，本行本外币个人贷款余额33.31亿元，比年初增加4.85亿元，增幅17.06%，在各项贷款中占比8.71%，比年初上升0.20个百分点。

3、对公存款情况：截至报告期末，本行本外币对公存款余额397.71亿元，比年初增加43.75亿元，增幅12.36%，在各项存款中占比67.49%，比年初下降0.96个百分点。

4、对公贷款情况：截至报告期末，本行本外币对公贷款余额349.23亿元，比年初增加43.44亿元，增幅为14.20%，在各项贷款中占比91.29%，比年初降低0.2个百分点。

##### Key Financial Indicators (Unit: RMB Yuan)


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Item</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Year 2014</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Year 2013</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Year 2012</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Earnings per share</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0.50</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0.61</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0.61</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Net assets per share</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3.71</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3.18</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2.95</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Net cash flow per share generated from operating activities</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>-0.41</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4.98</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2.24</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Rate of Return on net asset</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>14.17%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>19.16%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>22.50%</td></tr></table>

1. Rapid growth has been continuously maintained for the scale of assets. By the end of the report period, the total amount of assets of the Bank Group size was RMB 70.734 billion, increasing by RMB 9.666 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 15.83%; the total amount of standard assets of Parent Corporation legal person was RMB 69.873 billion, increasing by RMB 9.141 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 15.05%; the total amount of the Group's size loans was RMB 38.253 billion, increasing by RMB 4.829 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 14.45%; and the total amount of standard loans of Parent Corporation legal person was RMB 37.58 billion, increasing by RMB 4.479 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 13.53%.

2. The profitability growth gradually slows down. By the end of the report period, the standard profit before tax of the Group was RMB 1.031 billion, decreasing by RMB 1 million on year-on-year basis, at a decreasing rate of 0.13%; the standard profit before tax of Parent Corporation legal person was RMB 1.014 billion, decreasing by RMB 14 million on year-on-year basis, at a decreasing rate of 1.33%; the net standard profits of the Bank was RMB 763 million, decreasing by RMB 30 million on year-on-year basis, at a decreasing rate of 3.84%; and the net standard profits of Parent Corporation legal person was RMB 758 million, decreasing by RMB 34 million on year-on-year basis, at a decreasing rate of 4.27%. The return of equity of the bank is 14.17% and the return on assets is 1.16%.

3. Reasonable levels have been achieved in terms of cost control. During the report period, the ratio of standard cost and avenue of the Group was 36.70%, decreasing by 0.29% on year-on-year basis, among which, there was RMB 909 million business and management fees incurred, increasing by 13.79% on year-on-year basis; the operating income was RMB 2.477 billion, increasing by 14.71% on year-on-year basis; and the standard cost and avenue ratio of Parent Corporation legal person was 36.64%, decreasing by 0.03% on year-on-year basis, among which, there was RMB 890 million business and management fees incurred, increasing by 13.39% on year-on-year basis; the operating income was RMB 2.43 billion, increasing by 13.48% on year-on-year basis.

4. Operation risks have been controlled effectively. During the report period, facing the severe domestic and foreign economic situations, the Bank actively took measures and employed well-known consulting organization to carry out all-sided risk management system assessment planning project and line embedded risk management mode to further enhance the comprehensiveness and specialty; intensify credit risk management and control; strengthen industrial policy and industry research, improve the flow of credits and loans, emphasize on the development of SME loans, further decrease the credit concentration, optimize loan structure; enhance the clearing-out of non-performing assets and actively promote the quality of the credit assets. By the end of the report period, rate of the five-category's standard non-performing loans took up of 1.90%, decreasing by 0.05% from the beginning of the year; the provision coverage ratio was 155.62%, decreasing by 14.72% from the beginning of the year, and the capital adequacy ratio was 11.96%, and the core tier one capital adequacy ratio was 11.23%, respectively increasing by 1.1% and 1.4% from the same period of last year. By the end of the report, the lever ratio of the bank was 6.54%; the tier one net capital was RMB6.268 billion; the internal asset balance after regulation was RMB70.694 billion; and the external asset balance after regulation was RMB25.165 billion.

5. Institutional development strategy has been implemented successfully. During the report period, Weihai Branch, Xidajie Sub-branch in Xi'an, High-tech Sub-branch in Xi'an, Wudi Sub-branch in Binzhou, Xicheng Sub-branch in Dongying, Kenli Sub-branch in Dongying, Gaomi Sub-branch in Weifang, Luhu Sub-branch in Gaoqing and Shenbin Community Sub-branch in Binzhou were opened one after another; Institution building was accelerated continuously in the province, and institution coverage was expanded continuously outside the province.

6. Significant achievements in reform and innovation were obtained. During the report period, the institution building mechanism was obviously strengthened, various businesses were developed standard, and product innovation was further advanced; development efficiency of new products was further increased, the off-balance-sheet business was expanded more, and promotion and application of new products were widespread increasingly; line of financing products was established, and the products became rich increasingly, meeting the diverse needs of investors; building of financial service centers and professional branches for small business was sped up, intensity of marketing efforts was enhanced, and the differential and featured operation took new strides.

### II. Main Operation Information

1. Individual deposits: By the end of the report period, the balance of individual deposits of the Bank was RMB 19.161 billion, increasing by RMB 2.849 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 17.47%, which takes up 32.51% of various deposits, increasing by 0.96% from the beginning of the year.

2. Individual loans: By the end of the report period, the balance of individual loans of the Bank was RMB 3.331 billion, increasing by RMB 485 million from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 17.06%, which takes up 8.71% of various loans, increasing by 0.20% from the beginning of the year.

3. Corporate deposits: By the end of the report period, the balance of corporate deposits of the Bank was RMB 39.771 billion, increasing by RMB 4.375 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 12.36%, which takes up 67.49% of various deposits, increasing by 0.96% from the beginning of the year.

4. Corporate loans: By the end of the report period, the total amount of corporate loans of the Bank was RMB 34.923 billion, increasing by RMB 43.44 billion from the beginning of the year, at an increasing rate of 14.20%, which takes up 91.29% of various loans, decreasing by 0.20% from the beginning of the year.

## 二、年内召开股东大会情况

2014年4月30日，齐商银行股份有限公司2013年度股东大会在本行二楼群英厅召开，出席本次会议的股东和股东代表44名，代表有表决权股份数96,307.02万股，占总股本的74.08%，符合《中华人民共和国公司法》和公司章程的有关规定。董事长杲传勇先生主持了会议。会议经山东鲁杰律师事务所律师现场见证。

会议经过有效表决（同意44人，占出席会议股东或股东代表所代表股份数的100%，占总股本的74.08%），一致通过如下决议：

一、听取了《第五届董事会2013年度工作报告》；

二、听取了《第五届监事会2013年度工作报告》；

三、听取了《关于2013年度财务计划执行情况和2014年度财务预算草案的报告》；

四、审议通过了《关于2013年度利润分配方案的议案》，一致同意按总股本10%的比例派发红利，

其中：向全体股东每10股派送股票股利0.8股，共送股票股利104,000,000股，计104,000,000元；向全体股东每10股派送现金股利0.2元，共送现金股利26,000,000.00元，并决定将注册资本由13亿元变更为14.04亿元；

五、审议通过了《关于2014年增资扩股方案的议案》，一致同意定向募集新股份3亿股，并决定将注册资本由14.04亿元变更为17.04亿元；

六、审议通过了《关于修订公司章程的议案》；

七、审议通过了《关于制定<齐商银行股份有限公司监事会议事规则>的议案》；

八、审议通过了《关于同意王春汉先生辞去独立董事职务的议案》；

九、审议通过了《关于同意刘天林先生辞去股东监事职务的议案》。

大会还通报了《2013年度前十大股东持股情况》、《2013年度关联方交易情况》、《2013年度前十大授信集团客户情况》、《关于淄博银监分局审慎监管意见及我行贯彻落实情况的报告》、《监事会关于2013年度董事及高管层履职评价情况的报告》、《监事会关于2013年度监事履职评价情况的报告》。

2014年9月20日，齐商银行股份有限公司2014年第一次临时股东大会在本行二楼群英厅召开，出席本次会议的股东和股东代表共48名，代表有表决权股份数127,154.8万股，占总股本的74.62%，符合《中华人民共和国公司法》和公司章程的有关规定。董事长杲传勇先生主持了会议。会议经山东鲁杰律师事务所律师现场见证。

会议经过有效表决（同意48人，占出席会议股东或股东代表所代表股份数的100%，占总股本的72.27%），一致通过如下决议：

一、听取审议了《第五届董事会工作报告》；

二、听取审议了《第五届监事会工作报告》；

三、审议通过了《关于选举第六届董事会成员的议案》，选举马乃孝、王强、王德洋、孙守年、李全、张涛、张振焱、杲传勇、胡元木、赵晓东、徐书栋、鞠杰等十二位同志为第六届董事会成员；

四、审议通过了《关于选举第六届监事会成员的议案》，选举王林、刘庆国、刘丽珍、宫传洋、段福兴、韩兴柱等六名同志为第六届监事会成员；

五、审议通过了《关于修订<齐商银行股份有限公司股权管理办法>的议案》；

六、审议通过了《关于制定<齐商银行股份有限公司监事会工作实施细则>的议案》；

七、审议通过了《关于变更外部审计机构的议案》；

八、审议通过了《关于变更注册资本的议案》；

九、审议通过了《关于修订公司章程的议案》。

word-wrap: break-word;'>张振焱</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>职工董事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>齐商银行战略规划部总经理</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本科</td></tr></table>

## （二）董事会下设专门委员会

董事会下设战略规划委员会、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、薪酬考核及提名委员会、审计委员会、执行委员会，各委员会议事规则健全，报告期内共召开28次会议，各位委员出席率符合监管要求，充分有效地支持了董事会发挥决策职能。

## （三）董事会工作情况

报告期内，董事会认真落实监管部门完善公司治理的要求，不断加强董事会履职能力建设，公司治理的有效性得到进一步提升。一是持续完善公司治理制度。修订完善了《董事会对行长授权方案》，梳理并汇编形成了董事会层面的制度库，公司治理主体的职责边界和决策程序更加清晰。二是进一步加大对各类风险的管控力度。按照“管风险、管内控、定政策、把方向”的履职定位，定期听取研究信用风险等重点

领域风险管理状况，审议通过了《全面风险管理政策》，风险管理内控体系得到持续优化。三是重视并实施了战略规划的评估。组织实施了针对《2013-2015年三年发展规划纲要》贯彻执行情况的评估工作，进一步明确了目标完成情况、存在不足及今后的努力方向。四是履职能力建设进一步加强。组织董事会成员认真学习监管部门新颁布的《商业银行内部控制指引》等法规制度，举办了“2015年经济形势报告会”并组织董事会成员参加，及时传达和学习各级监管部门历次监管会议精神，作为董事会日常工作的有力指导，提高了自身的决策水平。五是董事会与监事会、高级管理层的协调运行机制进一步优化。董事会战略决策、高级管理层执行落实、监事会监督制衡的协调运行机制更加高效。六是持续加强资本管理。有效落实资本监管新规，在积极调整资产结构的基础上，创新采取股票股利的形式完成了2013年度红利分配工作，得到了广大股东的认可；于2014年7月完成了以3.3元的价格增发3亿股资本金工作，壮大了资本实力，保障了资本充足率持续达标。七是按时召开董事会例会及专门委员会会议。根据本行章程和股东大会授权，对职权范围内的事项进行认真讨论和审议，谨慎、认真、勤勉地履行了决策职责，年内共召开五次例会、一次临时会议，对44项议案进行了研究审议，同时听取了28项业务报告，并组织到西安分行、齐商村镇银行、小企业金融服务中心等分支机构开展现场调研。各位董事按照要求参加会议及调研活动，积极发表专业意见，忠实严谨，勤勉尽职，有效履行了各项职责，推进了本行董事会科学决策效率和水平的进一步提升。

## （四）独立董事工作情况


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>独立董事名</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本年应参加董事会数</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>出席次数</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>何德旭</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>胡元木</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>6</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>6</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>王春汉</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1</td></tr></table>

报告期内，三位独立董事依法按时参加了董事会会议。能够充分发挥从事金融专业研究的特长，及时分析研读财务报表，关注公司经营管理状况，适时针对经营风险控制、薪酬分配政策、跨区域经营、计划财务管理、董事会决议执行等法定关注事项，发表独立性意见，提出改进建议，履行独立监督职责。

## 四、监事会的构成及工作情况

## （一）监事会人员构成

本行监事会由6名监事组成，其中监事长1人，外部监事1人，股东监事2人，职工监事2人。


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>姓名</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>监事会职务</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>工作单位及职务</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>学历学位</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>韩兴柱</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>监事长</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>齐商银行党委副书记、监事长</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本科</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>段福兴</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>外部监事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>山东理工大学商学院党委书记</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>研究生</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>王 林</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>股东监事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>淄博新宇集团有限公司党委书记、董事长</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>研究生</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>宫传洋</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>股东监事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>山东传洋金属材料有限公司总经理</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本科</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>刘丽珍</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>职工监事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>齐商银行稽核审计部总经理</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本科</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>刘庆国</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>职工监事</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>齐商银行风险管理部总经理</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>本科</td></tr></table>

## （二）监事会工作情况

报告期内，监事会认真贯彻监管法规和公司章程，积极开展履职监督、财务监督、内控监督、风险管理监督活动，采取多项措施落实监督职责，持续改善监督方式，全面强化监督职能。按时召开监事会例会，审议通过了《齐商银行监事会2013年度工作报告》、《关于2013年度董事履职评价情况的报告》、《齐商银行监事会工作实施细则》等28个议案或报告；组织召开监事会监督委员会、提名委员会例会，研究审议有关应议事项；组织监事列席了五次董事会例会、参加年度股东大会。根据省、市银监局的监管意见及相关规章制度，修订完善了《齐商银行公司章程》和《齐商银行监事会工作实施细则（草案）》。对全行小微金融业务开展情况进行了巡视调研，形成了《关于小微金融业务发展情况的巡视监督意见》；对全行集团客户授信风险管理工作进行专项监督，形成了《关于集团客户风险管理状况的监督意见》；针对关联方及其交易管理状况，向董事会风险管理委员会发送了《关于对关联方及关联交易风险管理的监督意见》；针对操作风险管理工作状况，向高级管理层发送了形成了《关于对操作风险管理工作的监督意见》。报告期内各位监事均能按时参加监事会会议、列席董事会会议、出席股东大会，积极参加监督活动，有效履职尽责。职工监事能够按时参加经营管理会议，了解经营状况，维护员工合法权益。外部监事参加了五次监事会会议，主持了相关监事会专门委员会会议，列席了五次董事会会议，能够认真参与审议有关报告和提案，主动发表监督意见，依法履行监督职责。

## 五、高级管理层人员构成及基本情况

（一）本行总行高级管理层人员共有9人。其中行长1人，副行长4人，行长助理1人。董事会秘书1人，内审负责人1人，财务负责人1人。

（二）分支行高级管理人员共有分行行长6人，分行副行长13人，分行行长助理4人；支行行长87人，其中管辖支行行长29人，经营支行行长58人；管辖支行副行长37人，行长助理8人，经营支行副行长58人。

上述人员均符合监管部门规定的任职资格条件，并按要求参加了任职资格考试或备案。

## 六、部门和分支机构设置情况

本行董事会下设战略规划委员会、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、薪酬考核及提名委员会、审计委员会、执行委员会。监事会下设提名委员会、监督委员会。经营管理层总行下设19个部室及西安分行、滨州分行等94家分支行。

## 七、年度薪酬情况

2014年度本行董事会、监事会及高级管理人员履职情况良好，超额完成全年任务目标。本行董事、监事和高级管理人员在本行领取薪酬的共23人，共1232.94万元。

我行高级管理人员2014年度共发放绩效工资总计692万元。其中包括年度风险基金190万元，根据公司风险基金考核管理办法，该风险基金延后三年考核发放。

行内高级管理人员的薪酬根据季度考核结果按照实际得分情况兑现，独立董事、非执行董事、外部监事、股东监事的津贴根据尽职考核情况兑现。

董事会下设薪酬考核及提名委员会，独立董事担任主任委员，委员会成员拥有专业知识。薪酬考核及提名委员会按照《齐商银行股份有限公司董事会薪酬考核及提名委员会议事规则》履行职责，在董事会的授权下或经董事会批准后，负责制定、审批银行薪酬和绩效考核方案和制度，并监督其执行与落实。

#### II. General Meetings of Shareholders Held in This Year

On April 30, 2014, 2013 Annual Shareholders Meeting of Qishang Bank was held in the Elites Meeting Room on the second floor of the Bank: 44 shareholders/shareholder representatives attended this meeting, representing 963,070,200 shares with voting right, which take up 74.08 % of the total equity; therefore, holding of the meeting complied with relevant regulations of Company Law of the Peoples Republic of China and the Articles of Association of Qishang Bank. Mr. Gao Chuanyong, president of the Bank presided at the meeting, and lawyer from Shandong Lujie Law Office witnessed the meeting on site.

Through effective voting at the meeting, the following resolutions were unanimously adopted: (44 people agreed the resolutions, accounting for 100% of all shares represented by the shareholders or shareholder representatives at the meeting, which take up 74.08 % of the total equity):

1. Debriefed the 2013 Annual Work Report of the Fifth Session of Board of Directors;

2. Debriefed the 2013 Annual Work Report of the Fifth Session of Board of Supervisors;

3. Debriefed the Report on the Implementation of the 2013 Annual Financial Plan and the 2014 Financial Budget Draft;

4. Deliberated and passed Proposal on the 2013 Annual Profit Distribution Plan, and completely agreed on distributing bonus according to 10% of the total capital stock, including: distributing 0.8 stock dividend per 10 shares to all shareholders, 104,000,000 shares in total for stock dividend, namely RMB104,000,000;

distributing RMB0.2 cash dividend per 10 shares to all shareholders, RMB26,000,000.00 cash dividend in total, and decided to change the registered capital from RMB1.3 billion for RMB1.404 billion;

5. Deliberated and adopted the Proposals on 2014 Increasing Capital and Expanding Share and completely agreed on fixed direct collection of 300,000,000 new shares and decided to change the registered capital from RMB1.404 billion for RMB1.704 billion;

6. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Revising Articles of Association of the Company;

7. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Making Discussion Rules of the Board of Supervisors of Qishang Bank;

8. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Agreeing Mr. Wang Chunhan to Resign from Independent Director Post;

9. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Agreeing Mr. Liu Tianlin to Resign from Shareholders' Supervisor Post;

The meeting also informed Holding Situation of the Top 10 Shareholders in 2013, Related Party Transactions of the Shareholders in 2013, Client Information of the Top 10 Credit Group in 2013, Report on Prudential Supervision Views of Branch of Zibo Banking Regulatory Sub-bureau and the Implementation of Qishang Bank, Report of Board of Supervisors on the Evaluation of Directors' and Management Team's Duty Performance in 2013, and Report of Board of Supervisors on the Evaluation of Supervisors' Duty Performance in 2013.

On September 20, 2014, First Interim Shareholders' Meeting of Qishang Bank in 2014 was held in the Elites Meeting Room on the second floor of the Bank: 48 shareholders/shareholder representatives attended this meeting, representing 1,271,548,000 shares with voting right, which take up 74.62 % of the total equity; therefore, holding of the meeting complied with relevant regulations of Company Law of the Peoples Republic of China and the Articles of Association of Qishang Bank. Mr. Gao Chuanyong, president of the Bank presided at the meeting, and lawyers from Shandong Lujie Law Office witnessed the meeting on site.

Through effective voting at the meeting, the following resolutions were unanimously adopted: (48 people agreed the resolutions, accounting for 100% of all shares represented by the shareholders or shareholder representatives at the meeting, which take up 72.27 % of the total equity):

1. Debriefed the Work Report of the Fifth Session of Board of Directors;

2. Debriefed the Work Report of the Fifth Session of Board of Supervisors;

3. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Selecting Members of the Sixth Board of Directors and selected Ma Naixiao, Wang Qiang, Wang Deyang, Sun Shounian, Li Quan, Zhang Tao, Zhang Zhenyan, Gao Chuanyong, Hu Yuanmu, Zhao Xiaodong, Xu Shudong and Ju Jie, twelve people, as the members of the sixth board of directors;

4. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Selecting the Members of the Sixth Board of Supervisors and selected six people as the members of the sixth board of supervisors, namely Wang Lin, Liu Qingguo, Liu Lizhen, Gong Chuanyang, Duan Xingfu and Han Xingzhu;

5. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Revising Stock Right Management Method of Qishang Bank;

6. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Making Implementation Rules of the Work of the Board of Supervisors of Qishang Bank;

7. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Changing the External Auditing Organization;

8. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Changing the Registered Capital;

9. Deliberated and passed Proposals on Revising Articles of Association of the Company.

##### (II) Special Committees under the Board of Directors

Under the leadership of the Board of Directors, there are Strategic Planning Committee, Connected Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Remuneration Assessment and Nomination Committee, Auditing Committee, and Executive committee; each of them has sound and complete rules of procedure; during the report period, 28 meetings were held, of which the attendance rate of all committee members meets regulation requirement, which fully and effectively support the Board of Directors to perform its decision-making function.

##### (III) Work of the Board of Directors

During the report period, the Board of Directors seriously implemented the requirements of improving corporate governance by supervision authorities, continuously strengthened building of duty-performing capacity of the Board of Directors, so the efficiency of corporate governance was further enhanced. Firstly, the corporate governance system was constantly improved. The Rules of Procedure of Special Committees of the Board of Directors was revised and improved and system

database of the board level has been filed, summarized and compiled. So the boundary of responsibilities and decision-making process become clearer. Secondly, various risk management strength was further enhanced. According to the duty performing positioning of "risk management, internal control management, policy making, and direction confirming", the Board of Directors regularly debriefed and researched the situations of credit risk and other key areas of risk management, deliberated and adopted the Comprehensive Risk Management Policy and promoted the continuous improvement of internal control and risk management system. Thirdly, the evaluation on strategic planning was emphasized and implemented. The evaluation was carried out aiming at the implementation situation of the Outline of 2013-2015 Three-year Development Planning. The target completion situation, existing shortcomings, and the direction of future efforts were further defined clearly. Fourthly, the construction of duty performing ability has been further enhanced. The members of the Board were organized to earnestly study "Guideline to Internal Control of Commercial Banks" and other laws and regulations, newly issued by supervisory and administrative departments; "2015 Economic Situation Report Meeting" was held and members of the board of directors were organized to join in; the spirit of all regulatory conferences held by supervisory and administrative departments at all levels have been timely communicated and learned, as a powerful guide for daily work of the Board of Directors, and also improved their decision-making ability. Fifthly, the coordinated operating mechanism among the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, and senior management was further perfected. It is much effective that the board of directors decides the strategy and policy, the senior management executes the implementation and the board of supervisors monitors the balance coordinative operating mechanism. Sixthly, the capital management was continuously strengthened. New capital management regulations were effectively implemented, and based on the active asset structure adjustment, the innovative stock dividend way was adopted to compet 2013 bonus distribution, receiving acceptance from shareholders; capital fund of RMB300 million was added at a price of RMB3.3 in July 2014 to strengthen the capital power and protect that the capital adequacy ratio can satisfy the standard continually. Seventhly, Board Meetings were regularly held to fully perform its decision-making duty. During the period, authorized by the Articles of Association of Qishang Bank and the shareholders meeting, the fifth Board of Directors held five regular meetings and one extraordinary meeting to carefully discuss and deliberate 44 proposals and listened to 28 business reports, cautiously, carefully and diligently performing its decision-making functions; besides, the Bank organized on-site investigation and study to Xi'an Branch, Village Banks of Qishang Bank, and Small Enterprise Financial Service Center, and other branches. Each director participated the meetings and investigation and study activities as required, positively proposed their professional views; being faithful, rigorous, and diligent, they effectively perform each duty, thus promoted further enhancement of the efficiency and capacity of scientific decision-making of the Board of Directors of the Bank.

During the report period, three independent directors attended the meetings held by the Board of Directors pursuant to law and on time. They were able to bring into full play their expertise in research in the financial specialty and timely analyze financial statements. Concerned with the operation and management of the Company, they were able to deliver their independent opinions, put forward improvement suggestions and fulfill their responsibility for independent supervision with respect to statutory events for attention such as operation risk control, remuneration distribution policy, cross-regional operation, planning and financial management, and execution of Board of Directors' resolutions, etc.

## IV. Composition and Work of the Board of Supervisor

##### (I) Composition of the Board of Supervisors

The Board of Supervisors of the Bank is composed of 6 supervisors, including 1 Chief Supervisor, 1 External Supervisor, 2 Shareholder Supervisors, and 2 Employee Supervisors.

##### (II) Work of the Board of Supervisor

During the report period, the Board of Supervisors strictly executed supervision laws and regulations, actively implemented its supervision responsibilities such as performance supervision, financial supervision, internal supervision and risk management, adopted many measures to implement the monitoring duties and responsibilities, unceasely improved the methods of supervision, and comprehensively strengthened supervision functions. Rugular meeting of the board of supervisors has been convened on time to review and pass 28 proposals or reports such as 2013 Annual Work Report of Qishang Bank, Report on 2013 Annual Director's Performance Assessment Condition and Work Implementation Rules of the Board of Supervisors of Qishang Bank; the regular meeting of the monitoring committee and the nomination committee of the board of directors was organized and held to research and review relevant necessary discussion matters; the supervisors were organized to present at five regular meetings of the board of directors and annual shareholders' meeting. According to the regulatory opinion and relevant rules and systems of provincial and municipal bank regulatory bureaus, Articles of Association of Qishang Bank and Work Implementation Rules of the Board of Supervisors of Qishang Bank (Draft) have been revised and improved. Inspection tour and investigation were carried out to the development condition of the small & micro finance business of the bank and Inspection Tour and Supervision Opinion on the Development Condition of the Small & Micro Finance Business was formed; special supervision was carried out to client's credit risk management of the bank and Supervision Opinion on Group Client's Risk Management Status was formed; Supervision Opinion on the Affiliated Parties and Affiliated Transaction Risk Management was sent to the risk management committee of the board of the directors according to the management status of the affiliated parties and their transaction; Supervision Opinion on Operating Risk Management Work was formed and sent to senior management level according to the risk management operation status. During the report period, all supervisors were able to attend meetings of the Board of Supervisors on time, present at the board meetings and attended Shareholders' General Meeting, and carried out supervision and effectively performed their duties. The employee supervisors participated in meetings of senior management to timely grasp corporate operation information and effectively safeguard employees' rights and interests. The external supervisors participated five regular meetings of the Board of Supervisors, hosted the meetings of the relevant special committee of the Board of Supervisors, and attended five meetings of the Board of Directors. They carefully deliberated the related reports and proposals, delivered pertinent opinions or suggestions, and fulfilled their function of supervisors according to laws.

## V. Composition and Basic Information of Senior Management

(1) There are totally 9 members in the senior management of the Head Bank, including 1 president, 4 vice presidents and 1 president assistant, 1 secretary of the board, 1 internal audit chief and 1 finance chief.

(2) The Bank has 6 branch presidents, 13 branch vice presidents, 4 assistants for branch presidents; 87 sub-branch presidents, including 29 presidents of jurisdiction sub-branches and 58 presidents of operating sub-branches, 37 vice presidents of jurisdiction sub-branches and 8 assistants for branch presidents; 58 vice presidents of jurisdiction sub-branch.

All the above personnel meet the qualifications stipulated by the regulatory authorities and have participated in the qualification examinations or been filed on records.

## VI. Information about Setting of Departments and Branches

Under the leadership of the Board of Directors, there are Strategic Planning Committee, Connected Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Remuneration Assessment and Nomination Committee, Auditing Committee, and Executive committee. Under the Board of Supervisors, there are Nomination Committee and Supervising Committee. There are 19 divisions and departments, as well as 94 branches or sub-branches including Xi'an Branch, Binzhou Branch, and etc. under the leadership of the business management of head office of the Bank.

## VII. Annual Remuneration Information

In 2014, the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and senior management of the Bank performed their duty well, and therefore overfulfilled the task objective of the whole year. There are totally 23 members of the directors, supervisors and senior management of the Bank, who have received remuneration in the Bank, totaled RMB 12.3294 million.

The performance pay issued to the senior management totaled RMB 6.92 million Yuan in 2014, including RMB 1.90 million for annual risk fund, which will be paid three years later upon assessment according to the Measures for Administration and Assessment of Company Risk Fund.

The remuneration of senior management of the Bank was calculated based on their actual scores according to the quarterly assessment results. The subsidies of independent directors, non-executive directors, external directors and shareholder supervisors were calculated according to due diligence assessment.

Remuneration assessment and nomination committee is set under the board of directors. Independent directors occupy the principal member and the committee members shall have professional knowledge. The remuneration assessment and nomination committee shall perform duties and responsibilities pursuant to Discussion Rules of Remuneration Assessment and Nomination Committee of the Board of Directors of Qishang Bank and take charge of making and reviewing the remuneration and performance assessment resolution and system of the bank after obtaining the authorization or approval of the board of directors and monitor the execution and implementation.

二〇一四年度报告

2014 ANNUAL REPORT

# 第四部分 各类风险和风险管理策略 Part IV. Various Risks and Risks Management Strategies

## 第四部分 各类风险和风险管理策略

### 一、风险种类及形式

本行在经营中主要面临以下风险：信用风险、市场风险（包括利率风险、汇率风险等）、流动性风险、操作风险（包括结算风险、技术风险、系统风险等）、信息科技风险、声誉风险等。本行积极采取各种行之有效的措施，努力防范和化解上述风险。

（一）信用风险。本行在经营过程中，贷款、拆借、承兑、信用证等多种业务都可能存在和产生信用风险。

（二）市场风险。本行在经营中因市场汇率、利率变动而产生的风险。

（三）流动性风险。本行在经营中由于资产、负债变化形成的资金波动影响债权人实现债权而形成的风险。

（四）操作风险。本行由于内部程序、人员、系统的不完善或失误，或外部事件造成的风险。

（五）其他风险状况。其他可能对本行造成严重不利影响的风险因素。

## 二、风险管理策略

本行董事会下设风险管理委员会、关联交易控制委员会，高级管理层下设风险管理委员会，负责全行风险管理及控制。在各类风险管理和内部控制方面建立了符合现代银行监管要求的框架体系。总行有关部门根据职责分工具体负责全行信用风险、市场风险、流动性风险、操作风险、信息科技风险等的管理和控制。

### (一) 信用风险

2014年，本行注重强化信贷精细化管理，不断完善制度流程，进一步加快信贷结构调整步伐，多措并举强化授信风险管控，授信集中度进一步下降，实现了信贷资产结构的持续优化。一是信贷管理制度更加完善，增补修订了综合授信等二十余项授信管理制度，进一步细化了授权、调查、评级、授信、审批、放款、贷后等各环节和各岗位的主要风险点及管控措施，为深入推进信贷精细化管理提供了制度保障。二是信贷审批流程持续优化，制定了统一的授信操作流程，持续推进全年常态化综合授信，不断创新授信审查委员会审议模式，推行了客户经理与风险经理双线汇报制度，信贷业务审查审批效能不断提升。同时通过合理明确授权范围、积极运用信贷管理系统实时调整审批流程，实现了风险与效率的均衡兼顾。三是信贷客户分类管理制度全面实施。通过制定压降计划，严控受控受限行业客户授信，授信客户结构得到持续优化。四是信贷结构调整工作持续深入。通过细化完善授信政策，加大对“两高一剩”等重点关注行业新增授信的管控力度，引导分支机构从源头上把握好信贷投向。五是授信集中度明显好转。通过实施名单制管理、整体额度授信、限定授信产品等管理措施，集团客户管理效果明显。六是授信风险排查活动有序开展。组织开展了信用风险、票据业务、大额授信、“担保圈”授信风险、房地产行业等专项排查活动，逐

户进行风险分析，确定了潜在风险客户名单，对问题进行了认真分析、积极纠改和严格问责，为综合评估客户风险，彻底摸清风险底数，科学拟定风险处置方案奠定了基础。七是重视和推动绿色信贷工作。近年来，我行认真贯彻落实国家宏观调控政策和各项监管要求，根据《绿色信贷指引》要求，坚持“区别对待、有保有压”的原则，加强对全行授信客户特别是受限受控行业企业的风险管控力度，严控“两高一剩”行业信贷规模，持续加大对战略新兴产业、科技创新、节能环保领域信贷投放，信贷结构进一步优化。同时，积极引入国际先进的绿色信贷理念，与国际金融公司（IFC）就中国节能减排融资项目（简称CHUEE项目贷款）开展合作，进一步加强绿色信贷能力建设，建立健全绿色信贷标识和信贷制度，完善相关信贷管理系统，打造环境友好型的“绿色银行，低碳信贷”。

## （二）流动性风险

一是根据《商业银行流动性风险管理办法（试行）》、《齐商银行股份有限公司流动性风险管理政策》要求，对全行流动性风险管理的目标、保障措施、组织架构、董事会、监事会、高级管理层在流动性风险管理中职责、流动性风险的管理策略、政策和程序、流动性风险的识别、计量、监测和控制、信息系统建设和报告路径等方面逐一进行了梳理，为全行流动性管理提供了强有力的制度保障；二是成立全面风险管理体系建设工作领导小组，重点加强流动性风险管控；三是聘请知名咨询机构对全面风险管理体系进行评估与规划，其中重点对流动性风险管理体系进行了评估与规划，为提升流动性性风险管理水平提供了依据；四是制订了资本管理及补充规划，确立了资本管理战略，完善了资本管理体系和资本补充长效机制，保障了流动性管理水平和市场竞争力；五是根据全行资产结构和风险资产控制目标等合理制定债券投资等年度资产负债增长计划，保持充足流动性水平。2014年，本行每日收集分支行大额资金变动信息，根据资金头寸余缺情况，及时通过货币市场融通资金，兼顾流动性和效益性，做好日常头寸资金的预测和调度工作；每周做好大额资金变动监测，根据大额资金集中到期日，提前安排好资金期限；根据经济形势变化，以季度末数据为基础，模拟市场情景，精心设计流动性压力测试方案，进行流动性压力测试，年内成功举办了首次流动性风险应急演练，并在此基础上修订完善了本行流动性应急预案，进一步提高流动性管理能力。截至2014年末，本行集团口径流动性比例为65.84%，较年初上升1.87个百分点，始终保持在35%以上；未调整本外币存贷比64.91%，较年初上升0.27个百分点，控制在75%以内；母公司法人口径未调整本外币存贷比64.90%，较年初降低0.44个百分点，人民币超额备付率达到6.72%，超出监管标准1.72个百分点，各项流动性指标均保持较好水平。

## （三）市场风险

2014年，本行进一步完善了市场风险管理机制，设置了嵌入式风险管理人员，增强了风险管理的独立性；明确了市场风险容忍度指标，不断优化市场风险计量技术方法；每日撰写金融市场业务风险备忘录，对所持债券按照中债收益率曲线进行市值重估，随时关注债券利率走势及市场资金利率波动情况；每季度撰写市场风险管理状况报告，按季度对市场风险状况进行分析，及时调整投资策略，不断优化债券投资结构，全面监控评价市场风险。同时，加强市场研究，增强风险敏感度和防控能力；针对市场风险状况和外部市场发展情况以及不同类别、不同业务种类的市场风险，继续完善风险管理政策和程序，深入分析市场风险与其他风险类别的相关性，保持管控举措的协调性；针对单项业务产品的市场风险因素，完善市场风险识别、计量、监测和控制程序；定期对投资组合进行风险价值分析、久期分析和收益率曲线分析等，根据分析结果指导下一步投资，调整投资策略，规避市场风险，提高专业化分析水平。密切关注货币

政策取向，科学预测流动性风险及相关主体的信用风险、合理摆布资金期限结构，优化资金储备，提高资金收益率。对市场业务实行前、中、后台分离制约，严格执行“先审批后交易”流程，有效规避业务操作风险。防范因外币利率及汇率的不利变动所带来的市场风险，通过加强与境内外同业的交流合作、进一步完善相关业务制度、对业务情况进行定期检查等措施规避相关利率和汇率的市场风险。

## （四）操作风险

2014年，本行以深抓条线管控、严防操作风险为重点，不断完善制度及执行力建设，深入推进集中授权项目，操作风险管控力度显著增强，管控水平持续提升。一是深入开展会计结算辅导及飞行检查，有效防范操作风险。先后对异地分行、村镇银行进行了会计结算辅导，按季度完成全行案防及操作风险飞行检查，进一步摸清分支行操作风险现状，并结合检查中发现的问题，进一步完善了《问题库》，由2013年的153个问题点增加到199个，对分支行对照检查及整改起到了良好的指导作用。二是进一步完善制度建设，强化制度执行力。制定及修订下发《齐商银行柜员尾箱现金管理办法》、《齐商银行会计基本制度》、《齐商银行综合业务系统操作手册》等9项制度，制度体系建设进一步完善，制度执行力进一步强化。三是举办风险防范知识培训，提高风险防范意识。分别针对异地分行、全行柜员举办了“柜面业务风险防范知识专题讲座”及三期会计结算制度解读培训，增强了业务人员对风险点的识别和管控能力，强化了对业务制度的理解和掌握，有效增强了风险防范意识。四是集中授权系统稳步运行，柜面操作风险管控体系建设迈上新台阶。集中授权系统成功上线运行，将柜面业务风险管控重点前移，由事后监督向事中监督转变，增强了授权人员的独立性，有效提高了授权质量，进一步提升了柜面操作风险防控水平，为建立长效的柜面操作风险防控机制奠定了坚实的基础。

## （五）个人理财业务风险

在个人理财业务风险方面，本行严格按照《商业银行个人理财业务管理暂行办法》、《商业银行个人理财业务风险管理指引》、《商业银行理财产品销售管理办法》等相关制度要求开展理财产品的设计、备案、销售及管理，建立完善《人民币理财产品业务管理办法》、《人民币理财业务操作流程》等相关制度，确保理财业务的合规、稳健运行。

每期理财产品发行前，均进行详细的产品可行性研究，根据当时的市场状况与客户需求，设计开发符合客户利益和风险承受能力的理财产品，经监管部门备案后发售；产品销售过程中，认真进行客户投资取向分析，了解客户的财务状况、投资经验、投资目的，以及对相关风险的认知和承受能力，确保销售的理财产品符合客户利益和风险承受能力；产品运作过程中，按照监管要求，理财产品资产均委托具有证券投资基金托管资格的商业银行进行托管，理财资金封闭运作；产品成立、到期及存续期内，通过客服电话、短信、网站公告、营业网点等多种渠道，始终与客户保持及时、高效的沟通，使投资者随时了解理财产品的成立、到期及运作情况。理财产品的资金来源与运用一一对应，并且期限和当期产品严格匹配，有效防范了流动性风险。

## （六）科技信息风险

我行根据咨询机构信息科技风险专项审计报告提出的建议，一是设立了信息科技风险嵌入管理岗，印发了《业务连续性策略》、《业务连续性计划》、《业务连续性管理办法》、《业务连续性总体预案》

等文件的修改意见，初步完成了业务连续性管理体系建设工作；二是完成了与联盟间系统应急演练和内部重要信息系统应急演练；三是根据《淄博银监分局办公室转发银监会办公厅关于加强银行业金融机构信息科技非驻场集中式外包风险管理的通知》要求，完成《齐商银行关于信息科技非驻场集中式外包风险管理自查及整改情况的报告》；完成了两次信息科技项目上线及变更评审工作。从信息科技运行、信息系统开发、测试和维护、业务连续性管理方面，对2014年度信息科技管理进行风险检测、评估和风险提示，有效防范了信息科技风险。

## （七）声誉风险

2014年，本行进一步加大舆情监测管控力度，严格执行行内人员监测与外部协助监测相结合的工作方针，有效执行“7×24”小时监测制度，重点加大了涉及信贷风险和新业务可能引发舆情事件的重点监测，做到了全天候、全时段、全媒体的全面监测。一是通过开展声誉风险业务培训，提高全行新闻发言人及舆情监测员的思想认识和工作水平。二是高效利用外部资源，聘请专业机构开展舆情监测，不仅节省了人力成本，更提升了工作效能。三是加大对关键时间点和重要事件的舆情监测力度，做好及时发现、即时处置。根据上级监管部门相关文件规定，从2014年四季度监测情况分析，未发现涉及齐商银行的重大舆情事件，未出现重要负面舆情，声誉风险处于低风险可控状况。

## 三、全面风险管理组织架构图

# Part IV. Various Risks and Risks Management Strategies

### 1. Types and Forms of Risks

The Bank is mainly exposed to the following risks in operation: credit risks, market risks (including interest rate risks, exchange rate risks, etc.), liquidity risks, operation risks (including settlement risks, technical risks, system risks, etc.), information science and technology risks, reputation risks, etc. The Bank actively takes various effective measures to strive to prevent and resolve the above risks.

(1) Credit risks. In the course of operation, various businesses such as loans, inter-banking lending, acceptance and letter of credit may be subject to or give rise to credit risks.

(2) Market risks. The risks arise from fluctuation of exchange rate and interest rate in the market in operation.

(3) Liquidity risks. Liquidity risks are caused by the fund fluctuation due to the changes in assets and liabilities in the course of operation, which affect the realization of creditor's rights.

(4) Operation risks. Operation risks are caused by the imperfections or mistakes of internal procedures, personnel and systems, or external events.

(5) Other risks. There are other risk factors which may have adverse impact on the Bank.

#### II. Risk Management Strategies

Under the Board of Directors, the Bank has set up Risk Management Committee, and Connected Transaction Control Committee and risk management committee under senior management level, which are in charge of risk management and control of the whole Bank. A framework system meeting the modern banking regulatory requirements has been established for management of various risks and internal control. The related departments of the head office are responsible for the overall management and control of credit risks, market risks, liquidity risks, operation risks, and information science and technology risks of the whole Bank respectively according to the duty division.

##### (I) Credit Risks

In 2014, the bank paid attention to strengthening the credit and loan detail management and unceasingly improved the system flow, further accelerated the adjustment speed of the credit and loan structure, adopted many measures to strengthen the credit risk management and control and continually decrease the concentration ratio of the credit extension, which realized continual optimization of the credit-loan asset structure. Firstly, the credit-loan management system was much improved and more than twenty credit extension management systems such as comprehensive credit extension were added and revised, which further detailed the main risk points and management and control measures of all rounds and posts such as authorization, investigation, grading, credit extension, review and approval, making loan and post loan and provided system protection for deepening and advancing the credit-loan detail management. Secondly, the credit approval flow was continually optimized and unified credit extension operation flow was made to continually advance the whole-year normalized comprehensive credit extension; the review mode of the credit extension review committee was unceasingly innovated, double-line report system consisting of client manager and risk manager was launched, and credit business review and approval efficiency was continually promoted. Meanwhile, through rationally defining the authorization scope, actively applying credit management system and adjusting the review flow according to real time, the balance and equal attention to risk and efficiency was realized. Thirdly, credit client classification management system was carried out in all aspects. Through making pressure reduction plan, the credit of the clients under control or limit industry were strictly controlled and credit extension client structure was continually optimized. Fourthly, credit structure adjustment was continually deepened. Through detailing and improving credit extension policy, the management and control force on additional credit extension of key industries such as "high-pollution and high-consumption resource industries and one excess capacity industry" was strengthened and branches and sub-branches were guided to manage

the credit direction from the source. Fifthly, the credit extension concentration degree was obviously improved. Through implementing management measures such as name list management, overall credit limit and limited credit products, the group client management effect was obvious. Sixthly, credit risk inspection activity was carried out in order. Special inspection activities such as credit risk, note business, big amount credit, "credit risk in "guarantee circle" and real estate industry were organized and developed; risk analysis was carried out account by account to confirm the name list of clients with potential risk and complete careful analysis, active correction and strict accountability to lay foundation for comprehensively assessing the client risk, thoroughly finding out the risk base and scientifically drafting the risk resolution. Seventhly, green credit loan was emphasized and advanced. In recent years, our bank has carefully carried out and implemented the state macro-control policy and various supervision requirements and insisted on principles of "differential treatment and protection and suppression", strengthened the risk management and control force of the credit clients and especially enterprises under limitation and control, strictly controlled the credit scale of "high-pollution and high-consumption resource industries and one excess capacity industry", continually enlarged the credit extension in strategic emerging industry, scientific and technological innovation industry and energy-saving and environmental-protection infields according to the requirements of Green Credit Guidance. The credit structure was further optimized. Meanwhile, international advanced green credit idea was actively introduced in and cooperation with international finance company (IFC) on CHUEE Project Loan was carried out to further strengthen the construction of the green credit capacity, establish and improve green credit mark and credit system, improve relevant credit management systems and create environmental friendly "green bank and low-carbon credit".

##### (II) Liquidity Risks

Firstly, according to the requirements of Liquidity Risk Management Method of Commercial Bank (Trial Run) and Liquidity Risk Management Policy of Qishang Bank, the objective, protective measures and organization structure of liquidity risk management, board of directors, board of supervisors, duties and responsibilities of the senior level in liquidity risk management, management strategy, policy and procedure of liquidity risk, identification, measuring, supervision and control of the liquidity risk, construction and report path of information system of the bank were inspected one by one, providing powerful system protection to the liquidity risk of the bank; secondly, comprehensive risk management system construction and leading team was established and key attention was paid to strengthening the liquidity risk management and control; thirdly, well-known consulting organization was employed to assess and plan the comprehensive risk management system and especially pay attention to the assessment and planning of the liquidity risk management system, which provided reference for promoting the liquidity risk management level; fourthly, capital management and supplementation planning were made, capital management strategy was set and capital management system and capital supplementation long-term mechanism were improved to protect the liquidity management level and market competence; fifthly, annual asset-liability growth plans such as bonds investment were made rationally according to the asset structure and risk asset control purpose of the bank to keep adequate liquidity level. In 2014, the bank collected the large-amount capital change information of the branches and sub-branches every day. According to the fund position surplus and deficiency condition, the bank circulated necessary funds timely through currency market with concurrent attention to the liquidity and beneficial result and completed daily forecast and dispatch of the position fund; carried out dynamic supervision to large-amount fund every week; arranged fund deadline in advance according to the central deadline of large-amount fund; simulated market situation and well designed liquidity pressure test proposal based on the data by the end of the quarter according to the change of the economic situation to carry out liquidity pressure test; successfully held the first liquidity risk emergency drill within the year and revised and improved the liquidity emergency plan of the bank on such basis to further enhance the liquidity management capacity. By the end of 2014, the Bank's standard liquidity ratio was 65.84%, increasing by 1.87% over the beginning of the year, and always maintained above 35%; unadjusted the loan-deposit ratio of the foreign currency was 64.91%, increasing by 0.27% over the beginning of the year, and always maintained within 75%; the unadjusted loan-deposit ratio of the foreign currency of the legal person of the parent company was 64.90%, decreasing by 0.44% over the beginning of the year. The excess reserve rate of Renminbi of the legal person of parent company reached 6.72%, excessive by 1.72% over the supervisory standard. Various liquidity indexes were maintained at a good level.

##### (III) Market Risks

In 2014, the bank further improved market risk management mechanism and set embedded risk management personnel, which strengthened the independence

of the risk management; defined the market risk tolerance indicator and unceasingly optimized the market risk measuring technology and method; kept business risk memorandum of the financial market every day, reassessed the market value of the held bonds according to the yield curve of Chinese national debt and kept an eye on the bond interest rate trend and the fluctuation condition of the market fund interest rate; completed report on market risk management status every quarter, analyzed the market risk status every quarter, timely adjusted investment strategy, unceasingly optimized bonds investment structure and supervised the assessment market risk in all sides. Meanwhile, the bank strengthened market research and strengthened risk sensitivity and prevention and control capacity; continually improved risk management policy and procedure, carried deep analysis on the relevance between market risk and other risk types and kept the harmony of management and control measures in regards to market risk status, development condition of external market and market risks of different types and different businesses; improved the identification, measuring, supervision and control procedure of the market risk in regards to the market risk factors of single business product; carried out regular risk value analysis, long term analysis and yield curve analysis to investment combination; guided next-step investment according to the analysis result, adjusted investment strategy, avoided market risk and enhanced professional analysis level; kept close eye on the orientation of the currency policy, scientifically forecasted the liquidity risk and credit risk of relevant subjects, rationally arranged the capital deadline structure, optimized fund reservation and enhanced fund yield rate. Front, middle and backstage separation restriction shall be carried out to market business and "first review and later transaction" flow shall be strictly implemented to effectively avoid business operation risk. The market risk caused by the negative change of the interest rate and exchange rate of foreign currency shall be prevented. The market risk of relevant interest rates and exchange rates shall be avoided through measures such as strengthening domestic and overseas industrial communication and cooperation, further improving relevant business systems and carrying out regular inspection to business conditions.

#### (IV) Operation Risks

In 2014, the bank zeroed in on deeply carrying out line management and control and strictly preventing operation risk, continually kept improving the construction of system and executive force and went deep into advancing the central authorization project. As a result, the operation risk management and control force was obviously strengthened and the management and control level was continually promoted. Firstly, went deep into developing accounting settlement counseling and flight inspection to effectively prevent operation risk. The bank has successively carried out accounting settlement counseling to branches and village banks of the other place, completed the flight inspection on the safety protection and operation risk of the bank, further found out the status quo of the operation risk of the branches and sub-branches and further improved Problem Database by combining the problems found during inspection, increasing from 153 problems of 2013 to 199 problems, which played active guidance role to the comparison inspection and rectification of the branches and sub-branches. Secondly, further improved system construction and strengthened the executive force of the system. Nine systems such as Bank Teller's Cash Management Method in Tail Box of Qishang Bank, Basic Accounting System of Qishang Bank and Operation Manual of Comprehensive Business System of Qishang Bank were made, revised and issued. The system construction was further improved and the executive force of the system was further strengthened. Thirdly, held risk prevention knowledge training and enhanced risk prevention awareness. "Counter Business Risk Prevention Knowledge Theme Lecture" and three stages of introduction training on accounting settlement system were held to counter tellers of branches of another place and the bank, which strengthened the identification and management and control capacity of the businessmen when facing risk point, enhanced the understanding and master of the business system and effectively promoted the risk prevention awareness. Fourthly, central authorization system was steadily operated and the construction of the counter operation risk management and control system reached a new level. The successful online running of the central authorization system may lead to a result that the key points of the counter business risk management and control may move forward, changing from post supervision to middle supervision, strengthening the independence of the authorization personnel, effectively enhancing the authorization quality and further promoting the counter operation risk prevention and control level, which provided solid foundation to establish long-term counter operation risk prevention and control mechanism.

#### (V) Risk of Personal Financial Services

In aspect of individual financing business risk, the bank carried out design, recording, sale, and management for financing products in strictly accordance with the

requirements of Temporal Administrative Method for Personal Financial Services of Commercial Bank, Guidance for Risks Management of Personal Financial Services of Commercial Bank and Financial Product Sales Management Method of Commercial Bank and established perfect Administrative Method for RMB Financial Product Services, Operational Procedure for RMB Financial Services, and other relevant rules, to ensure compliance and stable running of financing business.

Before each financing product was issued, detail product feasibility research shall be carried out; designed and developed such financing products that conform to clients' benefits and interests and risk bearing ability according to market status and clients' demand at that time and sold the products after they were recorded by the regulatory authority. When selling the products, the Bank seriously analyzed clients' investment selection and direction, understood clients' financial positions, investment experience, and purpose and their recognition and bearing ability on relevant risks, to ensure that the financing products conform to clients' benefits and interests and risk bearing ability. When delivering the products, according to requirements from the regulatory authority, the financing assets were consigned to be trusted by the commercial banks that have qualification of trusting security investment fund. Financing capital was closed and operated. During the time when products were established, mature, and existing, the Bank kept timely and efficient communicate with clients by service call, message, announcement on the website, business station, and other ways to allow the investors to know about the establishment, maturity, and operation of financing products at any time. The fund source and application of the financial product shall realize one-to-one correspondence and the deadline shall strictly match the current-period product, which effectively prevented the liquidity risk.

##### (VI) Technological Information Risks

According to the opinions proposed by counseling organization in special information science and technology risk auditing report, our bank: firstly, established embedded information science and technology risk management post, printed the revision opinions of documents such as Business Continuity Strategy, Business Continuity Plan, Business Continuity Management Method and General Proposal on Business Continuity and initially completed the construction of the business continuity management system; secondly, completed system emergency drill and internal important information emergency drill in the alliance; thirdly, completed Report of Qishang Bank on Self Inspection and Rectification Condition of Non-stationed Central Outsourcing Risk Management of Information Science and Technology in accordance with requirements of the Notice of CBRC Office Transferred by CBRC Zibo Branch Office on Strengthening Non-stationed Central Outsourcing Risk Management of Information Science and Technology of Banking Financial Organization; completed the online and change review of twice information science and technology projects. In aspect of running of information science and technology, development, test and maintenance of information system and business continuity management, the bank carried out risk inspection, assessment and risk prompt to 2014 annual information science and technology management, which effectively prevented information science and technology risk.

##### (VII) Reputational Risks

In 2014, the bank further intensified the public opinion supervision management and control degree, strictly executed work guideline of combining internal personnel supervision and external assistant supervision, effectively implemented "7×24h" supervision system and paid special attention to enlarging the key supervision that had relevance to credit risk and new business and may arise public opinion event to realize all-sided supervision covering all situations, time and media. Firstly, the bank enhanced the ideological understanding and work level of the news spokesman and public opinion supervisor of the bank through developing reputation risk business training. Secondly, the bank made high effective use of external resources and employed professional organization to develop public opinion supervision, which not only saved human cost but also promoted work efficiency. Thirdly, the bank intensified the public opinion supervision degree at key time point and about significant events and realized disposal immediately after finding out. According to the requirements of relevant documents of upper-class supervision department and based on the analysis on the supervision conditions of four quarters of 2014, significant public opinion event relevant to Qishang Bank was not found out and serious negative public opinion was not found out. The reputation risk was under low-risk controllable status.

2014 ANNUAL REPORT

# 第五部分 本年度重要事项 Part V. Important Events of This Year

## 第五部分 本年度重要事项

## 一、报告期内，本行未发生对经营活动产生重大影响的诉讼、仲裁事项。

## 二、报告期内，本行无重大收购及吸收合并事项。

### 三、大事记

1月6日，齐商银行滨州无棣支行开业，标志着该行在滨州市的县域全覆盖。

1月25日，齐商银行召开2014年度工作会议，在全面总结2013年度工作情况的基础上，研究部署2014年的改革发展新任务、新举措。

齐商银行被评为“2013年度山东银行业信息宣传先进单位”、“2013年度淄博市银企合作信息网信息工作先进单位”。

3月19日，齐商银行召开党的群众路线教育实践活动动员大会，践行党的群众路线，着力解决“四风”问题。

3月21日，齐商银行芦湖支行顺利开业，是齐商银行在高青县成立的第三家支行。

3月28日，齐商银行东营西城支行开业。

3月31日至4月2日，齐商银行党委组织集中学习活动，党委成员率先系统学习党的群众路线理论。

4月3日，齐商银行召开第五届董事会第十次会议，同日召开监事会。

4月20日，齐商银行举办齐银学院揭牌暨第一期支行行长能力提升培训班开班仪式。

4月23日，党委书记、董事长、党的群众路线教育实践活动领导小组组长杲传勇到活动联系点——西安分行对群众路线教育实践活动进行工作指导。

自4月23日开始，齐商银行党委委员分别到联系点深入细致地开展群众路线教育实践活动指导。

4月30日，齐商银行股份有限公司2013年度股东大会在总行二楼群英厅召开。

5月14日，齐商银行西安西大街支行隆重开业，这是西安分行至今开设的第六家营业网点。

5月22日，齐商银行与博山区政府签订科学发展战略合作协议。

6月25日，齐商银行举行消防应急演练，通过火灾案例知识的学习与现场应急疏散演练，增强了员工的消防安全意识及突发事件的应急能力。

7月1日，齐商银行吉祥物“齐娃”创作完成并对外发布。

7月2日，齐商银行成功取得了北京金融资产交易所金融机构和理财计划代理人会员资格。

7月2日，齐商银行2014年首期新员工入职培训结业典礼在齐银学院举行。

7月9日，淄博市“市民卡·居民健康卡”项目签约仪式在齐商银行成功举行。

7月11日，中国中小商业企业协会游学团到齐商银行参观交流。

8月15日，齐商银行荣获全国“最佳中小企业服务奖”。

9月24日，齐商银行召开网点转型项目启动大会。

10月11日，齐商银行举行聘任仪式，正式聘任中国政法大学管晓峰教授为特邀法律顾问。

10月17日，齐商银行在齐盛国际宾馆召开党风廉政建设警示教育大会。

10月23日，齐商银行隆重召开全行党的群众路线教育实践活动总结大会视频会议。

11月28日，齐商银行在北京召开新闻发布会，对外宣布旗下互联网投融资平台——齐乐融融E平台上线运行。

12月2日，齐商银行西安高新科技支行开业。

12月17日，齐商银行第六家分行——威海分行开业。

12月20日，齐商银行潍坊高密支行在高密凤都国际大酒店举行揭牌仪式。

12月21日，齐商村镇银行滨河支行隆重开业。

12月26日，齐商银行组织召开2015年经济形势报告会，邀请到了财政部财政科学研究所研究员、中国财政学会绩效管理委员会执行秘书长李全博士，就2015年经济形势和相关政策进行专题讲座。

12月26日，齐商银行召开六届二次董事会和六届二次监事会。

12月28日，齐商银行东营垦利支行盛装开业。

12月29日，齐商银行首批内训师通关考核及聘任仪式在总行企业文化中心举行。

# Part V. Important Events of This Year

I. No lawsuit or arbitration that has significant influence on the Bank's operational activities occurred during the report period.

II. The Bank had no significant acquisition or merger during the report period.

## III. Important Events

On January 6, Wudi Sub-branch in Binzhou of Qishang Bank was opened, symbolizing the whole cover of the bank in the county of Binzhou City.

On January 25, Qishang Bank held 2014 Annual Work Meeting to research and arranged new tasks and measures for the reform and development of 2014 on the basis of summarizing annual work condition of 2013.

Qishang Bank was honored with "2013 Advanced Shandong Banking Information Publicity Unit" and "2013 Advanced Information Unit of Zibo Bank-Enterprise Cooperative Network".

On March 19, Qishang Bank convened mass line education and practice activity mobilization meeting of the party, practiced the mass line of the party and endeavored to resolve the problems on "formalism, bureaucratism, sensualism and extravagance".

On March 21, Luhu Sub-branch of Qishang Bank was successfully opened and is the third sub-branch of Qishang Bank established in Gaoqing County.

On March 28, Xicheng Sub-branch in Dongying of Qishang Bank was opened.

On April 3, Qishang Bank convened the tenth meeting of the fifth board of directors and held the supervisors' meeting on the same day.

From March 31 to April 2, the party committee of Qishang Bank organized massed learning activity and the members of the party committee were the first to systematically learn the mass line theory of the party.

April 20, Qisahng Bank convened the opening ceremony of the Qishang Bank College Unveiling Stage I Sub-branch President's Ability Promotion Training Class.

On April 23, Gao Chuanyong, secretary of the party committee, president and the leader of the mass line education and practice activity, came to the activity contact point-Xi'an Branch to guide the mass line education and practice activity.

Since April 23, the members of the party committee of Qishang Bank respectively came to the contact points to carry out deep and careful guidance to the mass line education and practice activity.

On April 30, 2013 Annual Shareholders' Meeting of Qishang Bank was held in Elites Room on the second floor of the head bank.

On May 14, Xidajie Sub-branch in Xi'an of Qishang Bank was grandly opened and is the sixth business site opened by Xi'an Branch so far.

On May 22, Qishang Bank and Boshan District Government concluded scientific development strategy cooperation agreement.

On June 25, Qishang Bank held fire emergency drill and strengthened the employees' fire protection safety awareness and emergency capacity through learning of fire case knowledge and emergency evacuation drill.

On July 1, the mascot creation of Qishang Bank was completed and released to the public.

On July 2, Qishang Bank successfully obtained the financial organization and financing plan agent member qualification of Beijing Financial Assets Exchange.

On July 2, 2014 First-stage New Employee's Orientation Training Completion Ceremony of Qishang Bank was held in Qishang Bank College.

On July 9, the signing ceremony of "Citizen Card·Resident's Health Card" of Zibo City was successfully held in Qishang Bank.

On July 11, the study group of China Association of Small & Medium Commercial Enterprises came to Qishang Bank for visit and communication.

On August 15, Qishang Bank was honored with national "Best Small & Medium Enterprise Service Reward".

On September 24, Qishang Bank convened site transfer project startup meeting.

On October 11, Qishang Bank held employment ceremony and formally employed Professor Guan Xiaofeng of China University of Political Science and Law as special legal consultant.

On October 17, Qishang Bank convened the caution and education meeting of the construction of the party conduct and of an honest and clean government in Qisheng International Hotel.

On October 23, Qishang Bank grandly convened the summarization video meeting of the mass line education and practice activity of the party of all industry.

On November 28, Qishang Bank convened press conference in Beijing and declared to the public that the subordinate internet investment and financing platform—Qilerongrong E Platform was operated online.

On December 2, Xi'an High-tech Sub-branch of Qishang Bank was opened.

On December 17, the sixth branch of Qishang Bank—Weihai Branch was opened.

On December 20, Gaomi Sub-branch in Weifang of Qishang Bank held unveiling ceremony in Gaomi Fengdu International Hotel.

On December 21, Binhe Sub-branch of Qishang Village Bank was opened grandly.

On December 26, Qishang Bank organized and convened 2015 Economic Situation Report Meeting and invited PhD Li Quan, researcher of Finance Science Research Institution of the Ministry of Finance and Executive Secretary of the Performance Management Committee of China Finance Society to give special lecture on 2015 economic situation and relevant policies.

On December 26, Qishang Bank convened the second meeting of the sixth board of directors and the second meeting of the sixed board of supervisors.

On December 28, Kenli Sub-branch in Dongying of Qishang Bank was opened grandly.

On December 29, the clearance examination and employment ceremony of the first group of internal trainers of Qishang Bank was held in the enterprise culture center of the head bank.

二〇一四年度报告

2014 ANNUAL REPORT

# Part VI. Financial and Accounting Report

## 第六部分 财务会计报告

### 一、审计报告

# 审计报告

# 信会师报字[2015]第530020号

齐商银行股份有限公司全体股东：

我们审计了后附的齐商银行股份有限公司（以下简称贵公司）财务报表，包括2014年12月31日的合并及公司资产负债表、2014年度的合并及公司利润表、合并及公司现金流量表、合并及公司所有者权益变动表以及财务报表附注。

### 一、管理层对财务报表的责任

编制和公允列报财务报表是贵公司管理层的责任。这种责任包括：（1）按照企业会计准则的规定编制财务报表，并使其实现公允反映；（2）设计、执行和维护必要的内部控制，以使财务报表不存在由于舞弊或错误导致的重大错报。

### 二、注册会计师的责任

我们的责任是在执行审计工作的基础上对财务报表发表审计意见。我们按照中国注册会计师审计准则的规定执行了审计工作。中国注册会计师审计准则要求我们遵守中国注册会计师职业道德守则，计划和执行审计工作以对财务报表是否不存在重大错报获取合理保证。

审计工作涉及实施审计程序，以获取有关财务报表金额和披露的审计证据。选择的审计程序取决于注册会计师的判断，包括对由于舞弊或错误导致的财务报表重大错报风险的评估。在进行风险评估时，注册会计师考虑与财务报表编制和公允列报相关的内部控制，以设计恰当的审计程序，但目的并非对内部控制的有效性发表意见。审计工作还包括评价管理层选用会计政策的恰当性和作出会计估计的合理性，以及评价财务报表的总体列报。

我们相信，我们获取的审计证据是充分、适当的，为发表审计意见提供了基础。

### 三、审计意见

我们认为，贵公司财务报表在所有重大方面按照企业会计准则的规定编制，公允反映了贵公司2014年12月31日的合并及公司财务状况以及2014年度的合并及公司经营成果和现金流量。
