## 三、财务报表附注

## 一、公司基本情况

(一) 行业性质：金融业。

## (二) 经营范围：

吸收公众存款；发放短期、中期、长期贷款；办理国内结算；办理票据承兑与贴现；发行金融债券；代理发行、代理兑付、承销政府债券；从事债券回购和现券买卖；从事同业拆借；提供担保；代理收、付款项及代理保险业务；提供保管箱服务业务；办理地方财政信用周转使用资金的委托贷款业务；办理经中国人民银行批准的外汇业务；办理借记卡、公务卡业务；办理网上银行业务；办理证券投资基金销售业务；办理理财业务；经中国人民银行、中国银行业监督管理委员会批准的其他业务。

本财务报表业经公司全体董事于2015年3月26日批准报出。

## (三) 合并财务报表范围

截止2014年12月31日，本公司合并财务报表范围内子公司如下：

##### 子公司名称

#### 临沂河东齐商村镇股份有限公司

详见“六、在其他主体中的权益”。

## 二、财务报表的编制基础

## (一) 编制基础

公司以持续经营为基础，根据实际发生的交易和事项，按照财政部颁布的《企业会计准则——基本准则》和具体会计准则、企业会计准则应用指南、企业会计准则解释及其他相关规定(以下合称“企业会计准则”)，以及中国证券监督管理委员会《公开发行证券的公司信息披露编报规则第15号——财务报告的一般规定》的披露规定编制财务报表。

## (二) 持续经营

公司自本报告期末至少12个月内具备持续经营能力，无影响持续经营能力的重大事项。

## 三、重要会计政策及会计估计

## (一) 遵循企业会计准则的声明

公司所编制的财务报表符合企业会计准则的要求，真实、完整地反映了报告期公司的财务状况、经营成果、现金流量等有关信息。

## (二) 会计期间

自公历1月1日起至12月31日止为一个会计年度。

## (三) 营业周期

本公司营业周期为12个月。

## (四) 记账本位币

本公司采用人民币为记账本位币。

## (五) 合并财务报表的编制方法

### 1、合并范围

本公司合并财务报表的合并范围以控制为基础确定，所有子公司（包括本公司所控制的单独主体）均纳入合并财务报表。

### 2、合并程序

本公司以自身和各子公司的财务报表为基础，根据其他有关资料，编制合并财务报表。本公司编制合并财务报表，将整个企业集团视为一个会计主体，依据相关企业会计准则的确认、计量和列报要求，按照统一的会计政策，反映本公司集团整体财务状况、经营成果和现金流量。

## (六) 外币业务和外币报表折算

### 1、外币业务

外币业务采用交易发生日的即期汇率作为折算汇率将外币金额折合成人民币记账。

### 2、外币财务报表的折算

资产负债表中的资产和负债项目，采用资产负债表日的即期汇率折算；所有者权益项目除“未分配利润”项目外，其他项目采用发生时的即期汇率折算。利润表中的收入和费用项目，采用交易发生日的即期汇率折算。

## (七) 金融工具

金融工具包括金融资产、金融负债和权益工具。

## (八) 长期股权投资

### 1、共同控制、重大影响的判断标准

共同控制，是指按照相关约定对某项安排所共有的控制，并且该安排的相关活动必须经过分享控制权的参与方一致同意后才能决策。本公司与其他合营方一同对被投资单位实施共同控制且对被投资单位净资产享有权利的，被投资单位为本公司的合营企业。

重大影响，是指对一个企业的财务和经营决策有参与决策的权力，但并不能够控制或者与其他方一起共同控制这些政策的制定。

### 2、初始投资成本的确定

(1) 企业合并形成的长期股权投资

(2) 其他方式取得的长期股权投资

### 3、后续计量及损益确认方法

#### (1) 成本法核算的长期股权投资

公司对子公司的长期股权投资，采用成本法核算。

##### (2) 权益法核算的长期股权投资

对联营企业和合营企业的长期股权投资，采用权益法核算。

#### (3) 长期股权投资的处置

处置长期股权投资，其账面价值与实际取得价款的差额，计入当期损益。

## (九) 固定资产

### 1、固定资产确认条件

固定资产指为生产商品、提供劳务、出租或经营管理而持有，并且使用寿命超过一个会计年度的有形资产。固定资产在同时满足下列条件时予以确认：

(1) 与该固定资产有关的经济利益很可能流入企业；

(2) 该固定资产的成本能够可靠地计量。

### 2、各类固定资产的折旧方法

固定资产折旧采用年限平均法分类计提，根据固定资产类别、预计使用寿命和预计净残值率确定折旧率。如固定资产各组成部分的使用寿命不同或者以不同方式为企业提供经济利益，则选择不同折旧率或折旧方法，分别计提折旧。

融资租赁方式租入的固定资产，能合理确定租赁期届满时将会取得租赁资产所有权的，在租赁资产尚可使用年限内计提折旧；无法合理确定租赁期届满时能够取得租赁资产所有权的，在租赁期与租赁资产尚可使用年限两者中较短的期间内计提折旧。

主要固定资产类别的折旧年限如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>资产类别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>折旧年限</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>房屋及建筑物</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>20年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>电子设备</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>机器设备</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>运输工具及其他</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5年</td></tr></table>

## (十) 在建工程

在建工程项目按建造该项资产达到预定可使用状态前所发生的必要支出，作为固定资产的入账价值。所建造的固定资产在建工程已达到预定可使用状态，但尚未办理竣工决算的，自达到预定可使用状态之日起，根据工程预算、造价或者工程实际成本等，按估计的价值转入固定资产，并按本公司固定资产折旧政策计提固定资产的折旧，待办理竣工决算后，再按实际成本调整原来的暂估价值，但不调整原已计提的折旧额。

## (十一) 无形资产

### 1、无形资产的计价方法

(1) 公司取得无形资产时按成本进行初始计量；

##### (2) 后续计量

在取得无形资产时分析判断其使用寿命。

### 2、无形资产的使用寿命估计情况

每期末，对无形资产的使用寿命及摊销方法进行复核。

### 3、无形资产减值准备的计提

对于使用寿命确定的无形资产，如有明显减值迹象的，期末进行减值测试。

对于使用寿命不确定的无形资产，每期末进行减值测试。

无形资产的减值损失一经确认，在以后会计期间不再转回。

## (十二) 长期资产减值

长期股权投资、采用成本模式计量的投资性房地产、固定资产、在建工程、无形资产等长期资产，于资产负债表日存在减值迹象的，进行减值测试。减值测试结果表明资产的可收回金额低于其账面价值的，按其差额计提减值准备并计入减值损失。可收回金额为资产的公允价值减去处置费用后的净额与资产预计未来现金流量的现值两者之间的较高者。资产减值准备按单项资产为基础计算并确认，如果难以对单项资产的可收回金额进行估计的，以该资产所属的资产组确定资产组的可收回金额。资产组是能够独立产生现金流入的最小资产组合。

## (十三) 其他资产

### 1、长期待摊费用

长期待摊费用是指已经支出但应由本期和以后各期负担的分摊期限在一年以上(不含一年)的各项费用。

### 2、抵债资产

抵债资产按取得时的公允价值入帐，公允价值与相关资产账面价值及支付的税费之间的差额计入当期损益。

抵债资产处置时，如果取得的处置收入大于抵债资产账面价值，其差额计入营业外收入；如果取得的处置收入小于抵债资产账面价值，其差额计入营业外支出；保管过程中发生的费用直接计入其他业务支出。处置过程中发生的费用，从处置收入中抵减。

### 3、其他应收款项

本公司按照其他应收款的项目和对方单位（个人）进行明细核算。本公司定期分析各项其他应收款项的可收回性，当应收款项的可收回金额低于其账面价值时，计提资产减值准备，并计入当期损益。

## (十四) 职工薪酬

### 1、短期薪酬的会计处理方法

本公司在职工为本公司提供服务的会计期间，将实际发生的短期薪酬确认为负债，并计入当期损益或相关资产成本。

本公司为职工缴纳的社会保险费和住房公积金，以及按规定提取的工会经费和职工教育经费，在职工为本公司提供服务的会计期间，根据规定的计提基础和计提比例计算确定相应的职工薪酬金额。

职工福利费为非货币性福利的，如能够可靠计量的，按照公允价值计量。

### 2、离职后福利的会计处理方法

##### (1) 设定提存计划

本公司按当地政府的相关规定为职工缴纳基本养老保险和失业保险，在职工为本公司提供服务的会计期间，按以当地规定的缴纳基数和比例计算应缴纳金额，确认为负债，并计入当期损益或相关资产成本。

除基本养老保险外，本公司还依据国家企业年金制度的相关政策建立了企业年金计划。本公司按职工工资总额的一定比例向年金计划缴费，相应支出计入当期损益或相关资产成本。

##### (2) 设定受益计划

本公司根据预期累计福利单位法确定的公式将设定受益计划产生的福利义务归属于职工提供服务的期间，并计入当期损益或相关资产成本。

### 3、辞退福利的会计处理方法

本公司在不能单方面撤回因解除劳动关系计划或裁减建议所提供的辞退福利时，或确认与涉及支付辞退福利的重组相关的成本或费用时（两者孰早），确认辞退福利产生的职工薪酬负债，并计入当期损益。

## (十五) 收入确认

收入在相关的经济利益很可能流入本公司且金额能够可靠计量，同时满足以下不同类型收入的其他确认条件时，予以确认。

### 1、利息收入

金融资产的利息收入根据让渡资金使用权的时间和实际利率在计入当期损益。利息收入包括折价或溢价的摊销，当实际利率与合同利率差异不大时，采用合同利率。

当一项金融资产或一组类似的金融资产确认减值损失后，确认利息收入所使用的利率为计量减值损失时对未来现金流进行贴现时使用的利率。

### 2、手续费和佣金收入

手续费和佣金收入在提供相关服务时，根据权责发生制的原则确认。

资产买卖、或参与第三方进行资产买卖交易（如购买客户贷款、证券，或出售业务）时产生的手续费于相关交易完成时确认。

### 3、股利收入

非上市权益工具投资的股利收入于本公司收取股利权力时确认计入当期损益，计入投资收益。

## (十六) 分部报告

本公司以经营分部为报告分部。经营分部是指该组成部分能够在日常活动中产生收入、发生费用；管理层能够定期评价该组成部分的经营成果，以决定向其配置资源；该组成部分的财务状况、经营成果和现金流量等有关会计信息能够取得。对提供单项或一组相关产品，风险及报酬相似的合并为一个经营分部。

## (十七) 重要会计政策和会计估计的变更

财政部于2014年颁布了下列企业会计准则，要求2014年7月1日起在所有执行企业会计准则的企业范围内施行。本公司已于2014年度执行这些新的企业会计准则，并按照相关准则中的衔接规定进行追溯调整，同时对2013年度财务数据进行了重新列报。

《企业会计准则第2号—长期股权投资》（修订）、《企业会计准则第9号—职工薪酬》（修订）、《企业会计准则第30号—财务报表列报》（修订）、《企业会计准则第33号—合并财务报表》（修订）]、《企业会计准则第37号—金融工具列报》（修订）、《企业会计准则第39号—公允价值计量》、《企业会计准则第40号—合营安排》、《企业会计准则第41号—在其他主体中权益的披露》。本公司执行上述修订的企业会计准则对本公司的影响如下：

### 1、《企业会计准则第2号—长期股权投资》（修订）

本公司根据长期股权投资的的定义，本公司将原持有的不具有控制、共同控制或重大影响，并且在活跃市场中没有报价、公允价值不能可靠计量的权益性投资，按照《企业会计准则第22号—金融工具确认和计量》处理，确认为可供出售金融资产，按成本法核算。该准则的修订对本公司财务报表的影响如下：

### 2、《企业会计准则第9号—职工薪酬》的修订

本公司根据有关短期薪酬、离职后福利、辞退福利和其他长期职工福利的分类、确认和计量等会计处理要求，对现有的职工薪酬进行了重新梳理，变更了相关会计政策。该准则的修订对本公司财务报表无重大影响。

### 3、《企业会计准则第30号—财务报表列报》的修订

根据《企业会计准则第30号—财务报表列报》（修订）的要求，本公司修改了财务报表中的列报，包括将利润表中其他综合收益项目分别以后会计期间在满足规定条件时将重分类进损益的项目与以后会计期间不能重分类进损益的项目进行列报等。

### 4、《企业会计准则第33号—合并财务报表》(修订)

根据《企业会计准则第33号—合并财务报表》(修订)的要求，本公司已变更相应的会计政策以确认对被投资方是否具有控制权。执行该准则不会改变本公司截至2014年12月31日止的合并范围。

### 5、《企业会计准则第37号—金融工具列报》（修订）

根据《企业会计准则第37号—金融工具列报》（修订）的要求，本公司重新梳理和修改了金融工具的列报，根据金融工具的特点及相关信息的性质对金融工具进行归类，并充分披露与金融工具相关的信息等。

### 6、《企业会计准则第39号—公允价值计量》

本公司已根据该准则的披露要求在本财务报告附注十二、5中进行了披露。执行该准则对本公司资产与负债的公允价值计量无重大影响。

### 7、《企业会计准则第40号—合营安排》

本公司已重新评估了参与合营安排的情况，并变更了合营安排的会计政策。执行该准则对本公司财务报表无重大影响。

### 8、《企业会计准则第41号—在其他主体中权益的披露》

本公司根据《企业会计准则第41号—在其他主体中权益的披露》的要求梳理评估了公司通过包括持有其他主体的股权、债权，或向其他主体提供资金、流动性支持、信用增级和担保等而可能享有其他主体中权益，采用该准则对本公司财务报表无重大影响。

## (一) 现金及存放中央银行款项


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>项目</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2014-12-31</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2013-12-31</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>库存现金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>403,118,470.23</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>471,824,206.23</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>存放中央银行法定准备金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10,029,546,003.25</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>8,476,485,269.35</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>存放中央银行超额存款准备金</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3,483,759,503.09</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3,212,837,958.98</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>存放中央银行财政性存款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>193,979,000.00</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>57,343,000.00</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>合计</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>14,110,402,976.57</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>12,218,490,434.56</td></tr></table>

缴存的法定存款准备金为按规定向中国人民银行缴存的存款准备金，此存款不能用于日常业务。2014年12月31日、2013年12月31日本公司人民币存款准备金缴存比率分别为17.5%、18%，2014年12月31日、2013年12月31日外币存款准备金缴存比率分别为5%、5%。存款范围包括机关团体存款、财政预算外存款、个人存款、单位存款、委托业务负债项目轧减资产项目后的贷方余额及其它各项存款。

子公司人民币存款准备金缴存比率列示如下：


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>被投资单位</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2014-12-31</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2013-12-31</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>临沂河东齐商村镇股份有限公司</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>14%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>14%</td></tr></table>

存放于中央银行超额存款准备金用于日常资金清算。

存放中央银行财政性存款是对国家金库款，地方财政预算内、外存款，部队、机关团体存款，财政部发行的国库券及各项债券款项等，按100%缴存中央银行的款项。

截止2014年12月31日，本公司可供出售金融资产不存在减值情况，不需计提可供出售金融资产减值准备。

2009年，长安银行股份有限公司经中国银行业监督管理委员会银监复（2009）212号《中国人民银行关于筹建长安银行股份有限公司的批复》批准，由宝鸡市商业银行股份有限公司等五家地方性银行金融机构通过新设合并，并由发起人共同发起设立，本公司作为发起人之一，于2009年4月在陕西省西安市签署发起人协议，以货币资金出资1.95亿元，认购长安银行股份1.95亿股，占股本总额的6.5%。

本公司向长安银行股份有限公司派驻高级管理人员，参与其日常业务经营，对其产生重大影响，因此采用权益法核算。

2014年12月30日，中国银监会陕西监管局[陕银监复(2014)90号]批准同意长安银行股份有限公司变更注册资本至4,070,172,977.00元；截至2014年12月31日，本公司持有长安银行股份有限公司223,236,000股，占股本总额的5.4847%。

2013年10月28日，经中国银监会下发《中国银监会关于齐商银行发行金融债券的批复》[银监复(2013)555号]文批准，同意本公司在全国银行间债券市场发行金额不超过20亿元人民币的金融债券；2014年4月9日，中国人民银行下发《中国人民银行准予行政许可决定书》[银市场准予字(2014)第24号]文依法作出准予行政许可的决定；2014年5月27日，本公司发行2014年第一期金融债券，债券代码1420010，主承销商为南京银行股份有限公司，发行总额人民币5亿元，发行价格100元/百元，票面利率5.9%。

本公司2013年度股东大会决议通过的2013年度利润分配方案，以总股本10%的比例派发股利，其中，向全体股东每10股派送股票股利0.8股、现金股利0.2元；根据2014年4月30日股东会决议及2014年7月22日经中国银行业监督管理委员会山东监管局[鲁银监准（2014）272号]核准，发行股票30000万股，每股面值1元，发行价3.3元。已于2014年7月31日经淄博鲁信会计师事务所有限公司审验，出具鲁信验字（2014）36号报告。本次增资扩股后注册资本为1,704,000,000元。

2015年3月26日，本公司第六届第三次董事会议通过本公司2014年度利润分配预案，按税后利润的10%提取法定盈余公积75,760,728.13元。

## 七、主要表外项目

## (一) 表外业务是指所有不在资产负债表中反映的业务，包括两部分：

或有风险的表外业务，即为客户债务清偿能力提供担保、承担客户违约风险的业务，如贷款承诺等；无风险的表外业务主要包括结算、代理业务。

## (二) 或有风险

1、银行承兑汇票是由承兑申请人向本公司申请，经本公司审查同意承兑的商业汇票。

2、信用证指本公司根据申请人的要求和指示，向收益人开立的载有一定金额，在一定期限内凭规定的单据在指定地点付款的书面保证文件。

3、银行保函指本公司应申请人或委托人的要求，以出具保函的形式向收益人承诺，当申请人不履行合同约定的义务或承诺的事项时，由本公司按保函约定履行债务或承担责任的信贷业务。

## (二) 诉讼事项

截止2014年12月31日，本公司存在正常业务中发生的若干未决法律诉讼事项。经考虑专业意见后，本公司管理层认为该等法律诉讼事项不会对本公司产生重大影响。

## 十、代理业务

## (一) 受托贷款及受托存款

本公司替第三方贷款人发放委托贷款。本公司作为中介人根据提供资金的第三方贷款人的意愿向借款人发放贷款，并与第三方贷款人签立合同约定负责替其管理和回收贷款。第三方贷款人自行决定委托贷款的要求和条款，包括贷款用途、金额、利率及还款安排。本公司收取委托贷款的手续费一般为一次性收费，但贷款发生损失的风险由第三方贷款人承担。


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>项目</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2014-12-31</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>受托贷款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3,835,822,206.27</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>受托存款</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3,843,981,456.27</td></tr></table>

## (二) 理财业务

本公司将金达创富人民币理财产品销售给个人和机构投资者，募集的资金主要运用于银行间市场债券投资、定向资产管理计划等，本公司从该业务中获取手续费、理财顾问服务费等收入。

## 十一、关联方关系及交易

## (一) 关联方

本公司的关联方包括持本公司5%及5%以上股份的股东，持本公司5%及5%以上股份股东的控股股东及其控股子公司，本公司董事、监事、高级管理人员及与其关系密切的家庭成员，因本公司董事、监事、高级管理人员及与其关系密切的家庭成员在本公司以外兼任董事长、总经理职务而与本公司构成关联关系的单位（以下简称“相同关键管理人员的其他企业”），本公司的子公司、合营公司、联营公司。

## 十二、资产负债表日后事项

2015年3月26日，本公司第六届第三次董事会议通过本公司2014年度利润分配预案，拟以2014年12月31日的股本为基数，向全体股东每10股发放股票股利1股并派送现金股利0.25元（含税）。该利润分配预案尚待股东大会审议通过。

除上述事项外，截止2015年3月26日，本公司不存在其他应披露的资产负债表日后重大事项。

缴存的法定存款准备金为按规定向中国人民银行缴存的存款准备金，此存款不能用于日常业务。

2014年12月31日、2013年12月31日本公司人民币存款准备金缴存比率分别为17.5%、18%。2014年12月31日、2013年12月31日外币存款准备金缴存比率分别为5%、5%；存款范围包括机关团体存款、财政预算外存款、个人存款、单位存款、委托业务负债项目轧减资产项目后的贷方余额及其它各项存款。

存放于中央银行超额存款准备金用于日常资金清算。

存放中央银行财政性存款是对国家金库款，地方财政预算内、外存款，部队、机关团体存款，财政部发行的国库券及各项债券款项等，按100%缴存中央银行的款项。

根据中国银行业监督管理委员会临沂监管分局《临沂银监分局关于同意临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司开业的批复》（银监临准[2010]123号），本公司向临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司出资1200万元，持股比例为60%；2012年，临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司注册资本增至5000万元，本公司出资增至2550万元，持股比例变更为51%。2014年，临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司注册资本增至10000万元，本公司出资增至5100万元，持股比例为51%。本公司作为临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司的第一大股东，按成本法核算。鉴于本行能够控制临沂河东齐商村镇银行股份有限公司的经营和财务决策，本行将其纳入合并财务报表范围。

## 十四、资本充足状况

### 1、资本充足率计算范围

包括本行以及符合中国银监会《商业银行资本管理办法（试行）》（自2013年1月1日1起实施）的本行直接或间接投资的金融机构。

### 2、资本数量、构成及各级资本充足率（集团口径）


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>项目类别</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2014年</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2013年</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>资本净额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>66.76亿元</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>48.87亿元</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>其中：核心一级资本</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>63.07亿元</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>44.38亿元</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>二级资本</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4.08亿元</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>4.60亿元</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>核心一级资本扣减项</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0.40亿元</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0.11亿元</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>因应用资本底线及校准后的风险资产合计</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>558.18亿元</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>450.20亿元</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>核心一级资本充足率</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>11.23%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>9.83%</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>一级资本充足率</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>11.23%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>9.83%</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>资本充足率</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>11.96%</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10.86%</td></tr></table>

### 3、风险加权资产计量

本行按照《商业银行资本管理办法（试行）》计量的风险加权资产情况：信用风险加权资产计量采用权重法、市场风险加权资产计量采用标准法，操作风险加权资产计量采用基本指标法。

### 4、本行实施新资本管理办法的情况

报告期内，本行按照《商业银行资本管理办法（试行）》要求，在明确办法推进的组织领导、职责分工和实施安排基础上，持续推进实施工作方案，聘请国内知名第三咨询机构完成了全面风险管理体系建设评估规划项目，持续完善以信用风险、市场风险、操作风险为重点的全面治理制度框架，定期对资本充足

状况进行检测、计算和分析，并对资本管理规划进行修订完善，确立了资本管理战略，采取股票股利的形式完成了2013年度股利分配，并完成了溢价增扩3亿股资本金工作，构建了资本管理及补充长效机制。同时，组织多次不同层级间的新资本管理办法的专题培训，加大了专业人才的储备和培养。

5、本行在资本管理方面遵循充分识别、计量、监测各类主要风险，2014年本行按季度对资本占用分配情况进行评估，并进一步优化资本配置及持续改进内部资本充足评估。

<div style="text-align: center;">十五、按五级分类不良贷款情况及不良资产管理的主要政策、措施及其效果（单位：万元）</div>



<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>不良贷款余额</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2014年12月31日</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>2013年12月31日</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>次级</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>64451.35</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>63536.45</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>可疑</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>8163.43</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1490</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>损失</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>33.02</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>合计</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>72614.78</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>65059.47</td></tr></table>

以加强风险预警为手段，持续加大不良贷款管理和清收力度，守住不良底线，加大问责力度，严防不良贷款出现反弹。采取的措施主要有：一是加强了风险贷款的常规化预警和调度管理，利用信贷管理系统实时发起客户风险预警，全行信息共享，实现对担保圈、资金链风险的即时拦截和处置。二是建立风险企业台账，指定专人每日监测不良及潜在风险贷款的变化和清收进展情况，实行名单制、销号制管理，严格实行动态监测，形成早识别、早预警、早处置的长效机制。三是开展“不良资产清收处置专项活动”，通过加强日常监测、层层签订目标责任书、加大考核力度、出台奖励政策、加大呆账核销力度等措施，加强存量不良贷款的清收处置工作。四是通过制定信用风险排查方案，对具有12项风险企业特征的客户，逐户逐笔摸清现状和成因，制定切实可行的清收压降方案，综合运用经济、行政、法律手段，灵活采取盘活、重组、诉讼措施，严防不良资产反弹。五是加大违规追责力度，2014年我行按照责任追究办法，处罚相关责任人299人次，处罚金额78.6万元。通过检查和问责，进一步增强信贷人员主动合规意识，有效防控信用风险。

## 十六、重要资产转让及出售

报告期内，本行购置固定资产支出7362.37万元，其中购置营业场所支出665.25万元、购置运输工具支出525.48万元、电子设备支出3003.67万元、其他资产支出3167.98万元；处置固定资产1288.38万元。

# Part VI. Financial and Accounting Report

### 1. Audit Report

#### Audit Report XKSBZ[2015]No.530020

To the shareholders of Qishang Bank Co., LTD

We have audited the financial statements of Qishang Bank Co., LTD (hereinafter referred to as "Qishang Bank" or "Company"), which comprise the consolidated and company's balance sheets as at December 31th, 2014, and the consolidated and company's income statement, cash flow statement and statement of changes in equity for the year then ended, and followed with the notes to financial statements.

#### i. Management's Responsibility for the Financial Statements

The management of the Company is responsible for the preparation and the true and fair presentation of these financial statements. This responsibility includes:

1. Selecting and applying appropriate accounting policies in accordance with Chinese Accounting Standards, and making accounting estimates that are reasonable in the circumstances.

2. Designing, implementing, maintaining internal control relevant to the preparation and the true and fair presentation of financial statements that are free from material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error.

#### ii. Auditors' Responsibility

Our responsibility is to express an opinion on these financial statements based on our audit. We conducted our audit in accordance with PRC Standards on Auditing issued by the Chinese Institute of Certified Public Accountants. Those standards require that we comply with ethical requirements, plan and perform the audit work to obtain reasonable assurance as to whether the financial statements are free from material misstatement.

An audit involves performing procedures to obtain audit evidence about the amounts and disclosures in the financial statements. The procedures selected depend on the auditors' judgment, including the assessment of the risks of material misstatement of the financial statements, whether due to fraud or error. In making those risk assessments, the auditors consider internal control relevant to the entity's preparation and fair presentation of the financial statements in order to design appropriate audit procedures rather than express an opinion on the effectiveness of the entity's internal control. An audit also includes evaluating the appropriateness of the accounting policies used by the directors and the reasonableness of accounting estimates made by the directors, as well as evaluating the overall presentation of the financial statements.

We believe that the audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our audit opinion.

iii. Opinion

In our opinion, the financial statements of Qishang Bank Co., LTD have been prepared in accordance with the Accounting Standards for Enterprises in all material respects, and give a fair view of consolidated and company's financial position of Qishang Bank Co., LTD as of December 31st, 2014 and its consolidated and company's operational performance and its consolidated and company's cash flow of 2014.

# III. Notes to Financial Statements

i. Basic Information of Company

(1) Nature of the industry: financial industry

## (2) Scope of business:

To take deposits from public; offer short-term, medium-term, and long-term loan; handle domestic settlement business; handle checks acceptance and discount; issue as agent, cashing, handle the sales of government bond; handle bonds buyback and trading of current bonds; handle inner-bank borrowing; provide guarantee; handle receipts and payments and handle insurance business; provide safe deposit box services; handle entrusted loan business of funds for local financial credit revolving; handle foreign exchange business approved by People's Bank of China; handle debit cards and business card business; handle Online Banking business; handle funds distribution business; handle financial services; other businesses approved by People's Bank of China and China Banking Regulatory Commission according to relative laws, regulations and other rules.

The financial statements shall be approved by the Board of Directors of the Bank on March 26, 2015.

## (3) Scope of consolidated financial statements

On December 31, 2014, the subsidiary in the Company's consolidated financial statements is listed as follows:


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Name of subsidiary</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>LinYi Hedong Qishang Village Bank Co., Ltd</td></tr></table>

Please see VI. Equity in other subjects for detail.

### ii. Basis for Preparation of Financial Statements

## (1) Basis for preparation of financial statement

The Financial Statements are based on accrual basis of accounting and a going concern basis, were prepared according to Accounting Standards for Enterprises — Basic Rules, specific accounting regulations, application guide and interpretation, and other relevant regulations (collectively referred to as "Accounting Standards for Enterprises"), as well as "The rules of company information disclosing for public securities No. 15th -- general provisions of financial reports" issued by China Securities Regulatory Commission.

## (2) Going concern

From the end of the reporting period, the company shall have the capability of operation for at least 12 months without any major affection on the Company's sustained operation.

### III. Major Accounting Policies and Accounting Estimates

## (1) Declaration on compliance with the Accounting Standards for Enterprises

The financial statements comply with the requirements of the Accounting Standards for Enterprises, truthfully and completely reflect the relevant information such as the Company's financial position, business performances, and cash flows, etc.

## (2) Accounting year

The financial year of the company runs from January 1 to December 31 of each calendar year.

## (3) Operating cycle

The operating cycle of the Company is 12 months.

## (4) Recording currency

The recording currency of the Company is Renminbi (RMB/CNY).

##### (5) Preparation of consolidated financial statements

#### 1. Scope of consolidation

A business combination is the bringing together of separate entities or businesses into one reporting entity, classified into the business combination under common control and business combination under non common control.

#### 2. Process of consolidation

The consolidated financial statements based on the financial statements of parent company and its subsidiary companies with several other related data. The consolidated financial statements takes the entire enterprise as one accounting unit, in accordance with related accounting standards for enterprise to determine, calculate and report; in accordance with unified accounting policy to report the entire company's consolidated financial situation, business results and cash flow status.

## (6) Foreign Currency Transactions and Foreign currency transformation

#### 1. Foreign currency transactions

Monetary items denominated in foreign currency are translated into RMB at the spot rate ruling at the transaction dates.

#### 2. Foreign currency transformation

Assets and liabilities of the balance sheets are translated at the spot exchange rate on the balance sheet date.

Except for "undistributed profits", other equity items are translated at the spot exchange rate ruling at the transaction dates. Translation differences are recognized in the income statement. Revenue and expense items of the income statement are translated at the spot exchange rate ruling at the transaction dates.

##### (7) Financial instruments

Financial instruments includes financial assets, financial liabilities and equity instrument.

##### (8) Long-term equity investments

#### 1. Determination of common control and significant influence

Common control refers to a sharing control that makes some arrangement according to the relevant agreements and the activities must be decided only after the participants who share control agree to each other.

If the company and other joint parties control the invested entity and share the rights of net assets, the invested entity is a joint venture of the company.

A significant influence, refers to the Company which has the right to participate in the decision-making on an enterprise's finance and operation, but cannot control the policy making by itself or with others.

#### 2. Determination of initial investment cost

(1) Long-term equity investment obtained by merging.

(2) Long-term equity investment obtained by other means.

## 3. Determination of subsequent measurement and income confirmation

(1) Long-term equity investment for subsidiaries calculated with cost method

(2) Long-term equity investment for associated enterprises and joint ventures calculated with equity method

(3) Disposing of a long-term equity investment

When disposing of a long-term equity investment, the difference between its book value and the actual purchase price shall be included in the profits and losses in the current period.

### (9) Fixed assets

1. Identification of fixed assets

Fixed assets are assets held by the Company for the conduct of producing, labor, leasing or business management and are expected to be used for more than one year. Fixed assets will be identified when meet the following requirement:

(1) Economic benefits related to these fixed assets are able to be combined to the enterprise;

(2) The cost of these fixed asset can be measured reliably.

## 2. Depreciation methods of various fixed

Depreciation of fixed assets is often determined by the straight-line method. The depreciation rate is determined according to the categories, estimated useful lives and expected net salvage value rate of fixed assets. If the service life of each component of fixed assets is different, or provide economic benefits for the company in different ways, different depreciation rates or methods should be chosen.

If the fixed asset is rent, and the ownership of the leasing asset after the term of lease expiration can be reasonably determined, this leasing asset can be depreciated within the useful life; if it is unable to determine whether the ownership of the leasing asset can be obtained after the term of lease expiration, this leasing asset can be depreciated within the shorter term of the lease term or the lease assets' useful life.

The depreciation year of main fixed assets is as follows:


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Fixed Assets Category</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Estimated Useful Life (Year)</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>House and Building</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>20</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Electrical Equipment</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>3</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Machinery and equipment</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>10</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Transportation facilities and others</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>5</td></tr></table>

### (10) Construction in Progress

Value of construction in progress accounts as the value of fixed assets is accordance with this construction project's intended costs before it is ready to use. If the construction project is ready to use, but haven't accounted for its completion, it can be transferred to fixed assets section with the estimated value, as soon as it reached its intended use date, and the value will be accounted according to the project budget, costs or the project's actual costs, and the depreciation method will be the same with the method of fixed assets. After accounts the completion of the project, Company will adjust the estimated value according to the actual costs, but will not adjust the accrual depreciation.

### (11) Intangible Assets

1. Pricing measurement of intangible assets

(1) Intangible assets are measured initially at cost;

(2) Subsequent measurement

Identified its useful life when having the intangible assets.

## 2. The estimation of intangible assets usage

At the end of each period, intangible assets will be reviewed on their using lives and amortization methods.

3. Depreciation and impairment of intangible assets

To intangible asset with certain useful lives, if have significant indication of impairment, text their impairment at the end of each term.

To intangible assets with uncertain useful lives, text their impairment at the end of each term.

As long as the impairment losses of intangible assets have been confirmed, they would not be reversed in subsequent accounting period.

### (12) Impairment of long-term assets

Those long-term assets such as long-term equity investment, real estate investment measured through cost measurement, fixed assets, construction in progress and intangible assets, will be taken impairment testing if there is any indication of impairment on the balance sheet date. If the test results show that the impairment of the asset's recoverable amount is lower than its book value, accords the differences on impairment allowance and record the differences onto impairment losses. The recoverable amount is the higher one between fair value of an asset minus the net amount of the disposal expenses and present value of assets' expected future cash flow. Impairment of assets is calculated and confirmed according to the recoverable amount of each asset, if it is difficult to ensure the recoverable amount of any monomial assets, estimate the recoverable amount of the assets group as the recoverable amount. Assets group is the smallest asset portfolio to produce cash inflows independently.

### (13) Other assets

## 1. Long Term Prepaid Expenses

Long term prepaid expenses refer to the prepaid expenses which are amortized over 1 year (not including 1 year).

## 2. Debt assets

Debt assets is recorded with the fair value at the acquisition, the differences between the assets' fair value and book value as well as related taxes and fees will be recorded as current profits and losses.

When dispose debt assets if the income of disposal is greater than the debt assets' book value, the differences shall be accounted into the non-operating profits; if the income of disposal is less than the debt assets' book value, the differences shall be accounted into the non-operating expenses; the expenses occur during storage is directly accounted into other operating expenses. Expenses occur during disposal will be deducted from the disposal income.

## 3. Other receivables

The Company accounts the subsidiary ledger in accordance with other receivables and other units (individuals). The Company regularly analyses the collect ability of other receivables. If the other receivables recoverable amount is less than its book value, the impairment of assets will be written into the current profits and losses.

### (14) Employee compensation

## 1. The accounting method of short-term remuneration

If employee serviced to the Company during the accounting period, Company shall recognize the short-term remuneration payable as liabilities, record into current profits and losses or related equity assets.

The social insurance, housing fund, union funds and employee training expenses are paid or drawn by Company, shall be confirmed with corresponding amount of remuneration according to regulation drawing basis and proportion in employee's servicing period.

If the remuneration are not monetary benefits, and can be measured reliably, accounts with fair value.

## 2. The accounting method of termination benefits

### (1) Enactment of draw and deposit plan

Company pays the basic old-age insurance and unemployment insurance in accordance with relevant local government provisions for the employees. During the accounting period with the employers' service, Company accounts the payment in accordance with local regulation base number and proportion, and confirms the payment as liabilities and records into current profits and losses, or into relevant costs of assets.

In addition to the basic old-age insurance, Company also established enterprise annuity plan in accordance with relevant national enterprise annuity policy. The Company carries out the annuity plan according to a certain percentage of employee's total wages, and the expense is included in current profits and losses, or into relevant costs of assets.

### (2) Defined benefit plan

The Company accounts welfare obligations, which is confirmed by expected cumulative welfare method, and occurred by benefit plan- in employee's service period, and the expense is included in current profits and losses, or into relevant costs of assets.

## 3. The accounting method of dismission benefits

If the Company cannot unilaterally withdraw the dismission benefits occurred on cancellation of labor relationship or proposal layoff, or cannot confirm the costs or expenses (confirm the one with earlier date) relate to involving the payment of dismission benefits, the Company will confirm termination benefits as liabilities on employee compensation, and record it into current profits and losses.

### (15) Revenue Recognition

When related economic benefits can be measured and are likely to flow into the company, and can also meet the following different types of income confirming conditions, the income will be confirmed.

## 1. Interest income

Interest income of financial instruments is recognized in profits or losses based on the time of using the transferred assets and the real interest rate.

Interest income includes the amortization of any discount or premium, when there are Minor differences between the real interest rate and the contract interest rate, use the contract interest rate.

When the impairment losses of a financial asset or a group of similar financial asset is confirmed, the rate using to confirm this interest income, is the rate used to measure future discounted cash flows on impairment losses.

## 2. Procedure fee and commission income

Procedure fee and commission income will be confirmed when offering related services according to the accrual basis.

Procedure fee of asset purchases or participating in the third party asset purchases such as purchasing client's loans, securities, or the sale business, will be confirmed after the relevant transactions completed.

## 3. Dividend income

Dividend income from unlisted equity investments is recognized in profit or losses on the date when the company collects dividend rights.

The dividend will be writing into investment income when the income of non-public company's equity instrument is confirmed to be included in the current profits and losses.

### (16) Segment reporting

The operating segment refers to a part of the Company, which can generate revenue and incurs expenses in daily activities; the management can periodically evaluate the component operating results, and determine its allocation of resources; and the related accounting information such as financial position, operating results and cash flow of this part can be obtained.

The parts that provide a single or a group of related products and have similar risks and rewards will be merged into a operating segment.

### (17) Important changes in accounting policies and accounting estimates

The ministry of finance issued the following accounting standards for enterprises in 2014 and required all enterprises within the scope of execution to implement these accounting standards since July 1, 2014.

The company has implemented these new accounting standards for enterprises in the year 2014 and traced the adjustment of cohesion in accordance with the relevant standards, as well as the 2013 annual financial data from the presentation.

The accounting standards for Enterprises No.2 - Long-term equity investment(Revised), The accounting standards for Enterprises No.9 - Employee remuneration(Revised), The accounting standards for Enterprises No.30 - Presentation of financial statements(Revised), The accounting standards for Enterprises No.33 - Consolidated financial statements(Revised), The accounting standards for Enterprises No.37 - Presentation of financial instruments(Revised), The Enterprise Accounting Standards No.39 - Fair value measurement, The accounting standards for Enterprises No.40 - Joint arrangements(Revised), The accounting standards for Enterprises No.41 - Disclosure of interests in other entities (Revised). The effects on the company due to implementing the revised accounting standards are as follows:

## 1. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.2 – Long-term equity investment (Revised)

According to the definition of long term equity investment, The company identified the original equity investment holdings that the company does not have control, joint control or significant influence and that has no price or fair value cannot be reliably measured in the active market as Available for sale financial asset by cost method, in accordance with The accounting standards for Enterprises No. 22 - Recognition and measurement of financial instruments. The impact of standards on the financial statements of the company is listed as follows:

## 2. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.9 - Employee remuneration (Revised)

The company revisits the accounting policies of classification, recognition and measurement in respect of employee benefits by categories of short-term employee benefits, termination benefits, postemployment benefits, and other long-term employee benefits. The adoption of this standard does not have any material impact on the Group's financial statements.

## 3. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.30 - Presentation of financial statements (Revised)

According to The accounting standards for Enterprises No.30 - Presentation of financial statements (Revised) requirements, the presentation of financial statements has been modified. Including making presentation on the items of other comprehensive income of income statement which would be reclassified to profits or losses in the future if certain conditions are met and those items that would never be reclassified to profits or losses.

## 4. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.33 – Consolidated financial statements (Revised)

According to The accounting standards for Enterprises No.33 - consolidated financial statements (Revised) requirements, the company has changed its accounting policy with respect to determining whether it has control over an investor. The implementation of the standards will not change the scope of the company until December 31, 2014.

## 5. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.37 - Presentation of financial instruments (Revised)

According to The accounting standards for Enterprises No.37 - Presentation of financial instruments (Revised) requirements, the company has reviewed and modified presentation of the financial instruments. The company has classified the financial instruments according to the characteristics of financial instruments and related information, and fully disclosed the relevant information of financial instruments etc.

## 6. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.39 - Fair value measurement

The Company has provided those disclosures in notes12 (5). The implementation of the standards does not have any material impact on the fair value measurements of the company's assets and liabilities.

## 7. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.40 - Joint arrangements (Revised)

The Company has reassessed the type of the joint arrangement in which it is involved and changes the accounting policies in respect of joint arrangement. The implementation of the standards does not have any material impact on the company's financial statements.

## 8. The accounting standards for Enterprises No.41 - Disclosure of interests in other entities (Revised)

According to The accounting standards for Enterprises No.41 - disclosure of interests in other entities (Revised) requirements, The company has reviewed and assessed interests in other entities through holding the other entities equity, debt, or providing for other entities funds, liquidity support, credit enhancement and security etc. The implementation of the standards does not have any material impact on the company's financial statements.

At the year of 2009, According to The approval of the setting up Chang'an bank co. LTD. by BRC, which is issued by the China Banking Regulatory Commission (BRC,NO.[2009]212), The Chang'an bank co. LTD. was established by five local commercial banks such as Baoji City Commercial Bank co., LTD. through the way of the new merger, and was established jointly by the promoters. As one of the promoters, the company signed the sponsor agreement in Shanxi Province, Xi'an City and invested RMB 195 million as monetary funds to purchase 195 million shares of Chang'an bank, which held the 6.5% of the share.

As the company has attached senior managements to Chang'an bank to participate in its daily business operation and can exercise significant influence over it, the company adopts the equity method.

On December 30, 2014, According to The approval of Chang'an bank to change its registered capital to RMB 4070172977.00 by BRC- Shan'xi Branch, which is issued by the China Banking Regulatory Commission, Shan'xi Branch(BRC- Shan'xi, NO.[2014] 90). As of December 31, 2014, the company held 223.236.000 shares of Chang'an bank co., LTD. and the shareholding ratio was 5.4847%.

On October 28.2013, CBCR approved our company to issue financial bonds no more than 2 billion RMB in the national inter-bank market through the paper <CBCR's approval of Qishang Bank to issue financial bonds > [CBCR (2013) No.555]. On April 9 2014, The People's bank of China made an administrative permission through the paper <The People's bank of China's administrative permission > [ bank market (2014) No 24]. On May 27, 2014, the company issued 2014 the first phase of financial bonds, bonds code is 1420010, and Bank of Nanjing Co., LTD., as the lead underwriter. Total issuance was 500 million Yuan, issue price was 100 Yuan/per hundred Yuan, the coupon rate was 5.9%.

Pursuant to the resolution of 2013 annual general meeting, profits distribution carried out at 10% of the capital stock to shareholders. Including: the stock dividend of 0.8 stocks and the cash dividend of RMB 0.2 Yuan per 10 stock will be distributed to all shareholders. According to the resolution of general meeting on April 30, 2014 and the approbation of CBRC- Shandong Branch's on July 22, 2014 (BRC- Shandong, NO.[2014] 272), the company issued 300 million shares, the par value per share was 1 yuan, the issue price was 3.3 yuan, which has verified in the "LuXinYanZi No. (2014) 36" Capital Verification Report by Shandong Zibo Luxin Accounting Firm Co., Ltd. The registered capital increased to RMB 1704000000.00.

Pursuant to related PRC regulations, the company is required to appropriate 10% of its annual net profits to the statutory surplus reserves.

In accordance with a resolution of board meeting on March 26, 2015, the Board of Directors proposed to appropriate 10% of the company's net profits for the year 2014 to the statutory surplus reserves in the amount of RMB 75760728.13.

The company further proposed an appropriation of RMB 29000000.00 to general risk reserve. Stock dividends (1 stock per 10 ordinary shares) and Cash dividends (RMB 0.25 before tax per 10 ordinary shares) to ordinary shareholders were also proposed. These appropriations are subject to final approval by shareholders.

## VII. Main off-balance sheet items

1. Off balance sheet business refer to business not reflected in the balance sheet, including two patrs:

The off balance sheet business of contingent risk is business of providing guarantee and bearing clients' risk of breach of contract with clients' debt solvency such as Loan Commitment, the off-balance sheet business without risk includes settlement and agency business.

### 2. Contingent risks

2.1 Bank acceptance bill is submitted by the acceptance applicant, and the company accepts after reviewed and agreed to it.

2.2 The Letter of Credit refers to a written warranty, which is based on applicant's requirement and instructions, write to beneficiary with a certain amount of money, and defined a certain period of time to make payment in a certain place with a certain bill.

2.3 Bank Letter of Guarantee also refers to a written warranty, and which is also based on applicant's requirement and instructions, but it guaranteed to beneficiary that, if the applicant refuse to fulfill the debt or response for the credit in accordance with all the agreement and commitment on the contract, the Company will do that.

### 2. Litigation matters

Until December 31.2014, there are several pending litigation matters arising in the course of normal business. After considering the professional opinions, the management of the company believes that the litigation matters will not have a significant impact on the company.

## X. Agency business

### 1. Entrusted loans and Entrusted deposits

The Company conducts entrusted lending arrangements for the third party lenders. Under the terms of entrusted loan arrangements, the Company grants loans to borrowers, as an intermediary, according to the instruction of the third party lenders who are the lenders providing the entrusted loans. On the basis of the contract signed with the third party lenders, The Company is responsible for the arrangement and collection of the entrusted loans. The third party lenders decide the requirements and conditions of Entrusted loans, including the loan purpose, amount, interest rate and repayment arrangements. The fee for Entrusted loans is generally a one-time fee, but the risk of the loan losses borne by the third party lender.

### XII Events after the Balance Sheet

On March 26, 2015, on the sixth board meeting for the third time the company approved the company's 2014 annual profits distribution plan.

The company is going to pay stock dividends (1 stock per 10 ordinary shares) and cash dividends (RMB 0.25 before tax per 10 ordinary shares) to all shareholders proposed by the equity base on December 31, 2014. The profit distribution plan is yet to be examined and passed at the general meeting of shareholders. In addition to the above event, by March 26th, 2015, the Group has not had any other significant event after the balance sheet date which should be disclosed.

Legal reserve fund deposited in Central Bank is the amount of reserve against deposit paid to Central Bank. The deposit can't be used for daily operating. The ratio of legal reserve fund deposited in Central Bank of our Company at December 31th, 2014, December 31th, 2013 is 17.5% and 8% respectively, the ratio of legal reserve fund deposited in Central Bank of foreign currency at December 31th, 2014, December 31th, 2013 is 5% and 5% respectively. The deposit includes the deposits of government agencies and organizations, deposits beyond budget, personal savings, corporate savings, and credit balance of debt less assets of the entrusted business and other various deposits. Overful deposited in the central bank deposit reserve funds will be used for daily liquidation.

With the central bank financial deposits of the state treasury, deposits of local financial budget of internal and external forces, deposits of government agencies and organizations, the bonds, funds and treasury bills issued by Ministry of Finance, paid 100% of the payment to Central Bank.

According to The approval of the opening of LinYi Hedong Qishang Village Bank Co., Ltd by CBRC-Linyi Branch, which is issued by the China Banking Regulatory Commission, Linyi Branch (BRC-Linyi, NO.[2010]123), the company invested RMB 12 million Yuan to LinYi Hedong Qishang Village Bank Co., Ltd, and held the 60% of the share; in 2012, the registered capital of LinYi Hedong Qishang Village Bank Co., Ltd was increased to RMB 50 million Yuan while the investment of the company increasing to RMB 25.5 million Yuan, therefore, the shareholding ratio changes to 51%. In 2014, the registered capital of Linyi Hedong Qishang Bank Co., Ltd increased to RMB 100 million Yuan while the investment of the company increasing to RMB 51 million Yuan, the shareholding ratio was still 51%. As the biggest shareholder of LinYi Hedong Qishang Village Bank Co., Ltd, the company measured through cost method. In view of the business and financial controlling of the village bank from the company, the company involved it into the scope of the financial report.

## XIV. Capital adequacy situation

## 1. The calculation range of capital adequacy ratio

The range covers the Bank and the financial institutions directly or indirectly invested by the Bank in accordance with the Capital Management Methods of Commercial Banks (trial), which was issued by the China Banking Regulatory Commission and implemented since January 1, 2013.

## 3. Risk-weighted assets measurement

The Risk-weighted assets of the Bank measured in accordance with the Capital Management Method of Commercial Banks (trial): The credit risk-weighted assets is measured by using the method of weighting, the market risk-weighted assets by using standard method, and operation risk-weighted assets by using basic indicator method.

## 4. The implementation of new capital management method

During the report period, in accordance with the requirements of the Capital Management Method of Commercial Banks (trial), based on clear definition of the organization and leadership, division of duties and responsibilities and implementation schedule in the Method, the bank continually implemented work plan and employed famous domestic third-party consulting organization to complete comprehensive construction assessment and planning of the risk management

system, continually improved the comprehensive management system framework with emphasis on credit risk, market risk, and operation risk; regularly inspected, calculated and analyzed the capital adequacy condition, revised and optimized the capital management planning and determined the capital management strategy; completed 2013 dividend distribution in the way of stock dividend, realized premium expansion of RMB 300 million stock capital and constructed long-term effective mechanism for capital management and compensation. At the same time, special training was carried out for several times for different levels in terms of new capital management method to strengthen the reservation and cultivation of professional talents.

5. With respect to capital management, the Bank fully identified, measured, and monitored various main risks. The bank carried out evaluation quarterly on capital occupation and distribution of the whole Bank in 2014 and further improved capital allocation and continuously optimized the assessment of the internal capital adequacy.

XV. Non-performing Loans Classified by Five-Category Asset Classification for Bank loans and Major Policy, Measures and Effects of Non-performing Assets Management (Unit: RMB Ten Thousand Yuan)


<table border=1 style='margin: auto; word-wrap: break-word;'><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Balance of non-performing loans</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>December 31, 2014</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>December 31, 2013</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Substandard</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>64451.35</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>63536.45</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Doubtful</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>8163.43</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>1490</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Loss</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>0</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>33.02</td></tr><tr><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>Total</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>72614.78</td><td style='text-align: center; word-wrap: break-word;'>65059.47</td></tr></table>

On the basis of strengthening the risk warning, the Bank continuously enhanced the control and management of non-performing loans, kept the permissible lowest level of non-performing loans, enhanced the accountability, and avoided the rebound of the non-performing loans. The Bank took the following measures mainly: firstly, the bank strengthened the normalized forewarning and dispatch management and made use of credit management system to generate client risk forewarning in real time; shared information in the whole bank to realize real-time interception and handling of warranty circle and capital chain risk. Secondly, the bank established enterprise risk accounting book and appointed special person to supervise the change and liquidation and collection of non-performing and potential risk loan everyday; carried out name list system and number cancellation system management; strictly carried out dynamic monitoring and formed long-term mechanism of early identification, early forewarning and early handling. Thirdly, the bank developed "special non-performing asset liquidation, collection and handling activities" and heightened the liquidation and handling of the inventory non-performing loan through such measures as reinforcing daily monitoring, signing target responsibility contract level by level, intensifying the assessment, launching award policy and reinforcing the bad debt verification and cancellation. Fourthly, the bank found out status quo and formation reason clearly of clients with 12 risk enterprise characteristics one by one through making credit risk inspection resolution, formulated practical and feasible liquidation, collection and suppression and drop resolution, comprehensively used economic, administrative and legal methods and flexibly adopted measures of vitalization, reorganization and litigation to strictly prevent the bouncing of non-performing assets. Fifthly, the bank increased the force of claiming for the liabilities for breach. In 2014, the bank punished 299 relevant person-in-charges and penalty of RMB 786,000 based on the responsibility investigation. Through inspection and accountability, the bank has further strengthened the active compliance awareness of the credit personnel and effectively prevented and controlled credit risk.

## XVI. Transfer and Sale of Important Assets

During the report period, the Bank's expenditure on the acquisition of fixed assets was RMB 73.6237 million, of which the expenditure on the purchase of business premises was RMB 6.6525 million, that on the purchase of transportation equipment RMB 5.2548 million, that on electronic equipment RMB 30.0367 million, and that on other assets was RMB 31.6798 million. The amount of disposed fixed assets was RMB 12.8838 million.
